### Gemini

Creating a complete, mathematically verified Fountain screenplay from scratch for a new series featuring Wonder Woman and Batman versus the Joker in Gotham City requires navigating the **Blessed Terminal Interface** across three coordinated repositories—**000.goblin-lore**, **000.goblin-spatial**, and **000.series-sower-engine**—before handing the compiled artifacts to **000.series-sower-conductor**. Because the current codebase contains a mix of live interactive generators and hardcoded demonstration fixtures, this guide first walks a new operator step-by-step through the exact terminal workflow and menu items, and then outlines every missing software capability that must be programmed to make a custom Gotham City production work end-to-end without manual code edits.

**Part 1: Beginner Orientation to the Three-Repository Pipeline and Terminal Cockpit**

The production system divides storytelling into three isolated local studios that share the immutable **apps/995.library** terminal harness:

- **000.goblin-lore (packages/299.lore):** Acts as the offline legislative authority where you create the series folder, define world physics, and author Markdown dossiers for characters, locations, props, and grievances. It compiles those files into a cryptographically sealed JSON contract named bible-state.json signed with a 64-character SHA-256 Merkle root hash.
- **000.goblin-spatial (packages/301.spatial):** Functions as the stateless physical geometry oracle operating on integer axial hex coordinates. It ingests room topology from the lore bible to verify whether characters can physically see, hear, or walk to one another.
- **000.series-sower-engine (packages/300.engine):** Serves as the sovereign simulation kernel and relational state ledger. It boots the sealed bible-state.json, runs the weighted conflict lottery and 2d6 duel math to decide what happens in a scene, enforces somatic and epistemic laws, and compiles the dialogue into a dual-artifact Fountain screenplay and Abstract Syntax Tree (dialogue_ast.json).
- **000.series-sower-conductor (packages/400.conductor):** Operates downstream as the master acoustic and visual orchestrator that ingests the finalized Fountain script and dialogue_ast.json to synthesize speech audio stems, quantize 24 frames-per-second timing budgets, and stamp out the episode timeline.

Every repository uses the **Blessed 12x12 Cockpit Dual-Pane Layout**:

- **Left Control Pane (Columns 0 to 4, Rows 4 to 12):** Displays the interactive vertical menu choices and pop-up text input boxes. You navigate up and down using the Up and Down arrow keys (or the k and j keys), press Enter to select a menu item, press Escape to cancel or step back, and press q or Ctrl+C to exit the terminal cleanly.
- **Right Information and Telemetry Pane (Columns 4 to 12, Rows 0 to 12):** Mounts the scrollable green-bordered console log widget (cns00) and status header, streaming real-time 7-bit ASCII receipts for every menu action you trigger.

**Part 2: Step-by-Step Menu Instructions from Scratch Genesis to a Finalized Fountain Screenplay**

**Stage 1: Breaking the Void State and Bootstrapping the Gotham Series in 000.goblin-lore**

1. **Launch the Lore Terminal Cockpit:** Open your system terminal in the 000.goblin-lore repository directory and start the Blessed interface, which initially boots into Posture A (the Cold-Start Byte-0 Void State) at zero compute cost with the status banner reading unbound and dispatch lockout engaged.
2. **Open the Lore Menu:** On the root switchboard in the left pane, highlight **LORE MENU** (or the hoisted root shortcut) and press Enter.
3. **Trigger Series Genesis:** Highlight the menu item **[G] UNIVERSAL SERIES GENESIS WIZARD** (also accessible via **[1] CREATE SERIES** in the genesis submenu) and press Enter.
4. **Enter the Series Slug:** When the yellow-and-black curses text box (84.input.unit) appears in the left pane prompting for a series name, type gotham-trinity-crisis and press Enter.
5. **Verify Automatic Folder and Seed Creation in cns00:** Watch the right-hand cns00 console pane as 29.series.unit and 27.seed.unit automatically create the hermetic folder tree under series/gotham-trinity-crisis/ (characters, locations, possessions, grievances, world, and ontology) and stamp series.config.json with the Universal Narrative 4-Tuple. Simultaneously, the bootstrap generator writes the initial Byte-0 seed dossiers—world_conditions_registry, world_economy_registry, loc_origin_hub, item_founder_token, char_protagonist, and grv_territorial_impasse—ensuring every file starts with the three-dash YAML frontmatter fence at physical byte offset zero.
6. **Optional Seed Audit:** To confirm the starter seeds are intact, select **[ 26. SCHEMA & INVARIANTS ]** from the Lore Menu and then select **[ 7. POPULATE GENESIS SEEDS ]**, which streams a seven-line verification receipt to cns00.

**Stage 2: Scaffolding Gotham City, Signature Props, Wonder Woman, Batman, Joker, and Grievances in 000.goblin-lore**

7. **Launch the Entity Scaffolder for Gotham Locations:** Return to the main **LORE MENU**, highlight **[S] ATOMIC ENTITY SCAFFOLDER** (or navigate into **[CHARACTER DOSSIER AUTHORING]** / **SCAFFOLD MENU**), and press Enter.
8. **Create the Gotham City Arena Dossier:** When the input modal prompts for an entity identifier, type loc_gotham_chemical_plant (the loc_ prefix automatically routes the file to the locations domain) and press Enter. Through the **Human Save Gate** (80.terminal.unit process suspension), the Blessed screen pauses at zero compute cost and opens your local text editor so you can define the Gotham location's closed hex boundaries, lighting in lux, and acoustic wall damping in centi-decibels (between 500 and 10,000 basis points).
9. **Scaffold Signature Props for the Cast:** Select **[S] ATOMIC ENTITY SCAFFOLDER** three times to create the physical possessions using the item_ prefix: enter item_lasso_of_truth (Weight tier, 1,500 grams, requiring one hand slot at 10,000 basis points), item_batarang_grapnel (Weight tier, 800 grams, requiring one hand slot at 10,000 basis points), and item_joker_venom_detonator (Scrap tier, 45 grams, requiring one hand slot at 10,000 basis points).
10. **Scaffold the Protagonist (Wonder Woman):** Select **[S] ATOMIC ENTITY SCAFFOLDER** (or **[CHARACTER DOSSIER AUTHORING]** followed by **[1] SCAFFOLD NEW CHARACTER** / **[2] SEED CORE GOBLIN CAST**) and enter char_wonder_woman. In the suspended editor gate, configure her Machine Rail frontmatter (location bound to loc_gotham_chemical_plant, two functional arms at 20,000 basis points, held inventory containing item_lasso_of_truth, high Vigor and Poise attributes, and a Refusal Vector stating she refuses to use lethal deception or abandon civilian hostages) and complete her five Voice Rail Markdown sections (Wants surface, Wants actual, Blind spot, Voice notes, and Few-shot lines).
11. **Scaffold the Deuteragonist (Batman):** Select **[S] ATOMIC ENTITY SCAFFOLDER** and enter char_batman. Configure his Machine Rail frontmatter (staged at loc_gotham_chemical_plant, two functional arms at 20,000 basis points, held inventory containing item_batarang_grapnel, high Cunning and Poise attributes, an epistemic horizon of Factual Witness, and a Refusal Vector forbidding lethal force and emotional vulnerability) and fill in his Voice Rail sections.
12. **Scaffold the Antagonist (The Joker):** Select **[S] ATOMIC ENTITY SCAFFOLDER** and enter char_joker. Configure his Machine Rail frontmatter (staged at loc_gotham_chemical_plant, two functional arms at 20,000 basis points, held inventory containing item_joker_venom_detonator, high Spite and Cunning attributes, and a Refusal Vector refusing to accept order or admit a punchline failed) alongside his Voice Rail sections.
13. **Scaffold the Active Gotham Grievance:** Select **[S] ATOMIC ENTITY SCAFFOLDER** and enter grv_gotham_chemical_standoff (the grv_ prefix routes to the grievances folder). Bind char_wonder_woman and char_batman against char_joker with an intensity rating between 1 and 5, defining the **Surface Dispute** (control of the chemical vat detonator and rooftop jurisdiction) and **The Underneath** (Batman's fear that his restraint enables Joker's atrocities versus Wonder Woman's warrior code).

**Stage 3: Auditing Invariants and Compiling the Sealed Canon in 000.goblin-lore**

14. **Run Schema and Anatomical SAT Audits:** In the **LORE MENU**, select **1. VALIDATE SCHEMA INVARIANTS**, **2. RUN SOMATIC SAT SOLVER**, and **3. AUDIT KINETIC MOTOR MASKS**. 17. **Seal the Canon and Mint the Merkle Root:** Select **[C] COMPILE CANON & GATE 0 GAUNTLET** to open the Compile Flight Deck, choose **[1] RUN COMPLETE THREE-PASS GAUNTLET**, and then select **[5] GATE 0 VERIFICATION AUDIT (ZERO-DRIFT TRIPWIRE)**. This writes compiled/gotham-trinity-crisis/bible-state.json with its 64-character SHA-256 Merkle root (H_root) and deep-freezes the world state. Press Escape and q to exit 000.goblin-lore.

19. **Load the Gotham Topology:** Select **Load Canonical Manifest** and **Inspect Active Topology Manifest** (plus **Inspect Coordinate Rings**) to load the Gotham Chemical Plant hex grid and verify portal reciprocity.

21. **Launch the Simulation Engine Cockpit:** Ensure compiled/gotham-trinity-crisis/bible-state.json is placed in the compiled/ directory of 000.series-sower-engine, launch the Blessed terminal, and select **SIMULATION ENGINE**.
22. **Boot the Canon Airlock:** Select **[01] BOOT ENGINE & ENSURE SCHEMA** (the Canon Airlock), choose the gotham-trinity-crisis manifest from the airlock submenu, and run **[03] SEED PRIMORDIAL GENESIS (S_0)**. 26. **Commit the State Transition:** Select **[11] AUDIT STAGING ACCUMULATOR & ATOMIC COMMIT** followed by **[14] EXECUTE DETERMINISTIC STATE FOLD & AUDIT WHY-DIRTY** to fold the duel outcome into the hash-chained event ledger and lock the scene's mandated exit state.
27. **Compile Epistemic Subtext Directives:** Select **[15] AUDIT EPISTEMIC HORIZONS & SUBTEXT ATTENUATION** to scrub unobserved world secrets from the prompt context (Ignorance by Omission) and anchor the scene's surface-versus-underneath subtext directive.
28. **Draft the Fountain Screenplay and Compile the Dialogue AST:** With the Spatial Stage Header (containing Gotham room coordinates, lighting lux, acoustic floor in centi-decibels, somatic limb limits, and the mandated exit beat) injected at Line 0 of the draft in drafts/, select **[16] COMPILE DUAL-FIELD DIALOGUE AST & ENFORCE CURTNESS** (and **[13] AUDIT UNRATIFIED DETAILS & SALIENCE HARVEST QUEUE** plus **COMPILE & PREFLIGHT PARALLAX DINNER**). This runs Pass 0 7-bit ASCII sanitization, checks every dialogue turn against the directorial curtness word budget, verifies that no action line uses a banned kinetic verb, asserts character-for-character verbatim parity between the .fountain script and dialogue_ast.json, and stages both files in drafts/.

### SYSTEM LAWS & ARCHITECTURAL INVARIANTS

1. **Strict Prohibition of `packages/299.lore/src/`:**
   - Under no circumstances shall any file be created under or imported from `packages/299.lore/src/`.
   - All code must reside strictly in its designated unit directories (`29.series.unit/`, `98.menu.unit/`, etc.) registered in `packages/299.lore/BEE.ts`.
2. **Immutable Host Harness Boundary (`apps/995.library`):**
   - No files inside `apps/995.library/` may be edited or modified. Any `git diff` across `apps/995.library/995.library/` must evaluate to exactly `0` lines.
   - All Blessed curses UI interactions (`81.grid.unit`, `83.console.unit`, `84.input.unit`, `85.choice.unit`) must be driven exclusively via `(globalThis as any).LIBRARY.hunt(...)` or `(global as any).LIBRARY.hunt(...)` calls from `packages/299.lore`.
3. **Pure 7-Bit ASCII Compliance:**
   - Zero multi-byte UTF-8 emojis or non-ASCII characters in source code, schemas, JSON configurations, test fixtures, or console telemetry logs.
   - All console telemetry strings emitted to `cns00` must use pure 7-bit ASCII tokens: `>>`, `[OK]`, `[WARN]`, `[FAIL]`, `::`, `---`.
4. **Integer Basis Points Standard ($0 \le x \le 10,000\text{ bp}$):**
   - Zero IEEE-754 floating-point numbers in state spaces or schema parameters.
   - All scarcity indices, farce ratio caps, and paranoia baselines must be integers ($10,000\text{ bp} \equiv 1.0$).
5. **NodeNext ESM Import Specifiers:**
   - All relative module import paths across `packages/299.lore/` must explicitly end with `.js` extensions.
6. **Title Invariant:**
   - Zero use of the word "AND" or the character "&" across all Phase, Feature, Task, and test suite titles.
7. **Asynchronous Reducer & Headless Fallback Resolution:**
   - All buzzer handlers execute asynchronously, preserve state immutability via `clone-deep`, resolve via `bal.slv({ ... })`, and gracefully fall back to deterministic defaults when `global.LIBRARY` is absent in headless Vitest runs.

```typescript
export type GenreClassification =
  | 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE'
  | 'STANDARD'
  | 'SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE'
  | 'SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL'
  | 'HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM';

export interface GenesisWizardStateSpace {
  readonly scarcity_index_bps: number;
  readonly paranoia_baseline_bps: number;
  readonly somatic_fatigue_enabled: boolean;
  readonly epistemic_ignorance_enabled: boolean;
  readonly gram_mass_tiers: {
    readonly scrap_max_grams: number;
    readonly weight_max_grams: number;
    readonly bulk_min_grams: number;
  };
}

export interface GenesisWizardActionVocabulary {
  readonly permitted_kinetic_verbs: readonly string[];
  readonly forbidden_verbs_global: readonly string[];
  readonly locomotion_taxonomies: readonly string[];
}

export interface GenesisWizardInvariantRules {
  readonly max_farce_ratio_bps: number;
  readonly min_grievance_depth_delta: number;
  readonly global_acoustic_floor_cdb: number;
  readonly voiceover_ducking_db: number;
  readonly min_normalized_category_entropy_bps: number;
  readonly thematic_categories: readonly string[];
}

export interface GenesisWizardMediumProjections {
  readonly target_fps: 24;
  readonly audio_sample_rate_hz: 48000;
  readonly tts_service_port: number;
  readonly video_service_port: number;
  readonly aspect_ratio: '16:9';
}

export interface AssembledFourTupleConfig {
  readonly schema_version: '1.0.0';
  readonly series_slug: string;
  readonly display_name: string;
  readonly genre_tag: GenreClassification;
  readonly central_irony: string;
  readonly state_space: GenesisWizardStateSpace;
  readonly action_vocabulary: GenesisWizardActionVocabulary;
  readonly invariant_rules: GenesisWizardInvariantRules;
  readonly medium_projections: GenesisWizardMediumProjections;
}

export interface BlessedChoiceModalOptions {
  readonly promptHeader: string;
  readonly choices: readonly string[];
  readonly defaultValue?: string;
}

export interface BlessedInputModalOptions {
  readonly promptLabel: string;
  readonly defaultValue?: string;
  readonly sanitizeSlug?: boolean;
}

export interface ModalPromptResult<T string> {
  readonly cancelled: boolean;
  readonly value: T;
}
```

#### 2. Reusable Blessed Modal Helpers: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/blessedModal.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import type {
  BlessedChoiceModalOptions,
  BlessedInputModalOptions,
  ModalPromptResult,
} from '../fce/genesisWizard.interface.js';

const UPDATE_GRID = '[Grid action] Update Grid';
const OPEN_CHOICE = '[Open action] Open Choice';
const OPEN_INPUT = '[Open action] Open Input';
const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console';

export function resolveLibraryBus(): any {
  return (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY || null;
}

export function sanitizeSeriesSlug(raw: string, fallback = 'ashfall-basin'): string {
  const cleaned = String(raw || '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .trim()
    .replace(/[\s_]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, '')
    .replace(/-+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
  return cleaned.length > 0 ? cleaned : fallback;
}

/**
 * [Task 1.1.1] Reusable 85.choice.unit modal invocation anchored to the left 4-column grid.
 */
export async function promptBlessedChoice(
  options: BlessedChoiceModalOptions,
): Promise<ModalPromptResult<string>> {
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  const fallbackChoice =
    options.defaultValue || (options.choices.length > 0 ? options.choices[0] : '');

  if (!lib || typeof lib.hunt !== 'function') {
    return { cancelled: false, value: fallbackChoice };
  }

  await lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, {
    idx: 'cns00',
    src: `>> [WIZARD:PROMPT] ${options.promptHeader}`,
  });

  const gridBit: any = await lib.hunt(UPDATE_GRID, {
    x: 0,
    y: 4,
    xSpan: 4,
    ySpan: 8,
  });

  const choiceList = [...options.choices, '[CANCEL]'];
  const choiceBit: any = await lib.hunt(OPEN_CHOICE, {
    dat: {
      clr0: 'black',
      clr1: 'yellow',
    },
    src: 'vertical',
    lst: choiceList,
    net: gridBit?.grdBit?.dat,
  });

  const selected = choiceBit?.chcBit?.src ?? '';
  const exitVal = choiceBit?.chcBit?.val ?? 0;

  if (exitVal === -1 || selected === '[CANCEL]' || selected === '') {
    return { cancelled: true, value: '' };
  }

  return { cancelled: false, value: selected };
}

/**
 * [Task 1.1.3] Reusable 84.input.unit text modal invocation with slug sanitization.
 */
export async function promptBlessedInput(
  options: BlessedInputModalOptions,
): Promise<ModalPromptResult<string>> {
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  const fallbackText = options.defaultValue || '';

  if (!lib || typeof lib.hunt !== 'function') {
    const val = options.sanitizeSlug
      ? sanitizeSeriesSlug(fallbackText, fallbackText || 'ashfall-basin')
      : fallbackText;
    return { cancelled: false, value: val };
  }

  await lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, {
    idx: 'cns00',
    src: `>> [WIZARD:INPUT] ${options.promptLabel}`,
  });

  const gridBit: any = await lib.hunt(UPDATE_GRID, {
    x: 0,
    y: 4,
    xSpan: 4,
    ySpan: 4,
  });

  const inputBit: any = await lib.hunt(OPEN_INPUT, {
    txt: options.promptLabel,
    src: fallbackText,
    dat: {
      clr0: 'green',
      clr1: 'cyan',
    },
    net: gridBit?.grdBit?.dat,
  });

  const exitVal = inputBit?.putBit?.val ?? 0;
  if (exitVal === -1) {
    return { cancelled: true, value: '' };
  }

  const rawEntered = (inputBit?.putBit?.src ?? '').trim() || fallbackText;
  const finalValue = options.sanitizeSlug
    ? sanitizeSeriesSlug(rawEntered, sanitizeSeriesSlug(fallbackText, 'ashfall-basin'))
    : rawEntered;

  return { cancelled: false, value: finalValue };
}
```

#### 3. Sequential Four-Tuple Wizard Orchestrator: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/genesisMenu.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type { MenuModel } from '../menu.model.js';
import type MenuBit from '../fce/menu.bit.js';
import * as ActSer from '../../29.series.unit/series.action.js';
import * as ActSed from '../../27.seed.unit/seed.action.js';
import { resolveRepoRoot } from '../../29.series.unit/buz/scaffoldSeriesTree.buzz.js';
import { seedDirectoryExamples } from '../../27.seed.unit/buz/seedDirectoryExamples.buzz.js';
import {
  promptBlessedChoice,
  promptBlessedInput,
  resolveLibraryBus,
  sanitizeSeriesSlug,
} from './blessedModal.buzz.js';
import type {
  AssembledFourTupleConfig,
  GenreClassification,
} from '../fce/genesisWizard.interface.js';

export function sanitizeAscii(raw: string): string {
  return String(raw || '').replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '').trim();
}

function emitConsole(ste: State | undefined, line: string): void {
  const cleanLine = sanitizeAscii(line);
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    lib.hunt('[Console action] Update Console', {
      idx: 'cns00',
      src: cleanLine,
    });
    return;
  }
  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    ste.hunt('[Console action] Update Console', {
      idx: 'cns00',
      src: cleanLine,
    });
  }
}

const GENRE_CHOICE_MAP: Record<string, GenreClassification> = {
  '[1] LOW FANTASY FARCE (Default)': 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
  '[2] STANDARD DRAMATIC REALISM': 'STANDARD',
  '[3] SUPERHERO URBAN VIGILANTE': 'SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE',
  '[4] SOUTHERN GOTHIC INDUSTRIAL': 'SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL',
  '[5] HARD SCIFI VACUUM': 'HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM',
};

const STATE_SPACE_PRESETS: Record<
  string,
  { scarcity_index_bps: number; paranoia_baseline_bps: number }
> = {
  '[1] HIGH SCARCITY (8500 bp / Paranoia 6500 bp)': {
    scarcity_index_bps: 8500,
    paranoia_baseline_bps: 6500,
  },
  '[2] URBAN CRISIS (6000 bp / Paranoia 8000 bp)': {
    scarcity_index_bps: 6000,
    paranoia_baseline_bps: 8000,
  },
  '[3] BALANCED BASELINE (5000 bp / Paranoia 5000 bp)': {
    scarcity_index_bps: 5000,
    paranoia_baseline_bps: 5000,
  },
};

const ACTION_VOCAB_PRESETS: Record<
  string,
  {
    permitted_kinetic_verbs: string[];
    forbidden_verbs_global: string[];
    locomotion_taxonomies: string[];
  }
> = {
  '[1] CONSTRAINED SCAVENGER (LIMP, SIPHON, CONCEAL)': {
    permitted_kinetic_verbs: ['LIMP', 'SIPHON', 'CONCEAL'],
    forbidden_verbs_global: ['fly'],
    locomotion_taxonomies: ['BIPEDAL_HUNCHED'],
  },
  '[2] HEROIC KINETIC (FLY, GRAPNEL, LASSO_BIND, GLIDE, DEFLECT, DETONATE)': {
    permitted_kinetic_verbs: [
      'FLY',
      'GRAPNEL',
      'LASSO_BIND',
      'GLIDE',
      'DEFLECT',
      'DETONATE',
    ],
    forbidden_verbs_global: [],
    locomotion_taxonomies: ['BIPEDAL_STANDARD', 'AERIAL'],
  },
  '[3] STANDARD TACTICAL (STRIKE, DEFLECT, CONCEAL, SPRINT)': {
    permitted_kinetic_verbs: ['STRIKE', 'DEFLECT', 'CONCEAL', 'SPRINT'],
    forbidden_verbs_global: ['fly'],
    locomotion_taxonomies: ['BIPEDAL_STANDARD'],
  },
};

const INVARIANT_PRESETS: Record<
  string,
  { max_farce_ratio_bps: number; global_acoustic_floor_cdb: number }
> = {
  '[1] STRICT DRAMA (Farce <= 1500 bp / Floor 1500 cdB)': {
    max_farce_ratio_bps: 1500,
    global_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1500,
  },
  '[2] NOIR ACOUSTIC (Farce <= 1000 bp / Floor 2200 cdB)': {
    max_farce_ratio_bps: 1000,
    global_acoustic_floor_cdb: 2200,
  },
  '[3] ELEVATED SATIRE (Farce <= 2500 bp / Floor 1200 cdB)': {
    max_farce_ratio_bps: 2500,
    global_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1200,
  },
};

/**
 * [Task 1.1.2] Sequential Four-Tuple Parameterization Wizard (<S, A, I, R_m>).
 * Chains 84.input.unit and 85.choice.unit modals before invoking 29.series.unit.
 */
export const runSeriesGenesisMenu = async (
  cpy: MenuModel,
  bal: MenuBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<MenuModel> => {
  const dat = (bal?.dat || {}) as Record<string, any>;
  const abortWizard = (reason: string): MenuModel => {
    const cancelLine = `>> [GENESIS:CANCELLED] ${reason}`;
    emitConsole(ste, cancelLine);
    cpy.activeSelection = '[G] UNIVERSAL SERIES GENESIS WIZARD';
    cpy.lastLifecycleReceipt = '[GENESIS:CANCELLED]';
    cpy.statusMessage = cancelLine;
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        mnuBit: {
          idx: 'open-genesis-menu',
          val: 0,
          dat: { cancelled: true, receipt: cancelLine },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  };

  emitConsole(ste, '>> [GENESIS:WIZARD] Initiating Sequential 4-Tuple Parameterization...');

  // Step 1: Series Slug via 84.input.unit (unless explicitly pre-supplied in payload)
  let seriesSlug = dat.seriesSlug ? sanitizeSeriesSlug(dat.seriesSlug) : '';
  if (!seriesSlug) {
    const slugRes = await promptBlessedInput({
      promptLabel: 'Step 1/6: Enter POSIX Series Slug (e.g. gotham-trinity-crisis)',
      defaultValue: 'ashfall-basin',
      sanitizeSlug: true,
    });
    if (slugRes.cancelled) {
      return abortWizard('Aborted at Step 1 (Series Slug).');
    }
    seriesSlug = slugRes.value;
  }

  const displayName =
    dat.displayName ||
    seriesSlug
      .split('-')
      .map((w) => (w.length > 0 ? w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1) : w))
      .join(' ');

  // Step 2: Genre Modality via 85.choice.unit
  let genreTag: GenreClassification = dat.genreTag || 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE';
  if (!dat.genreTag && !dat.config) {
    const genreOptions = Object.keys(GENRE_CHOICE_MAP);
    const genreRes = await promptBlessedChoice({
      promptHeader: 'Step 2/6: Select Genre Classification',
      choices: genreOptions,
      defaultValue: genreOptions[0],
    });
    if (genreRes.cancelled) {
      return abortWizard('Aborted at Step 2 (Genre Classification).');
    }
    genreTag = GENRE_CHOICE_MAP[genreRes.value] || 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE';
  }

  // Step 3: Tuple Element <S> State Space via 85.choice.unit
  let stateSpaceSelection = STATE_SPACE_PRESETS['[1] HIGH SCARCITY (8500 bp / Paranoia 6500 bp)'];
  if (!dat.stateSpacePreset && !dat.config) {
    const stateOptions = Object.keys(STATE_SPACE_PRESETS);
    const stateRes = await promptBlessedChoice({
      promptHeader: 'Step 3/6: Configure <S> State Space Accumulators',
      choices: stateOptions,
      defaultValue: stateOptions[0],
    });
    if (stateRes.cancelled) {
      return abortWizard('Aborted at Step 3 (State Space <S>).');
    }
    stateSpaceSelection = STATE_SPACE_PRESETS[stateRes.value] || stateSpaceSelection;
  }

  // Step 4: Tuple Element <A> Action Vocabulary via 85.choice.unit
  let actionVocabSelection =
    ACTION_VOCAB_PRESETS['[1] CONSTRAINED SCAVENGER (LIMP, SIPHON, CONCEAL)'];
  if (!dat.actionVocabPreset && !dat.config) {
    const actionOptions = Object.keys(ACTION_VOCAB_PRESETS);
    const actionRes = await promptBlessedChoice({
      promptHeader: 'Step 4/6: Configure <A> Bounded Action Vocabulary',
      choices: actionOptions,
      defaultValue: actionOptions[0],
    });
    if (actionRes.cancelled) {
      return abortWizard('Aborted at Step 4 (Action Vocabulary <A>).');
    }
    actionVocabSelection = ACTION_VOCAB_PRESETS[actionRes.value] || actionVocabSelection;
  }

  // Step 5: Tuple Element <I> Invariant Laws via 85.choice.unit
  let invariantSelection =
    INVARIANT_PRESETS['[1] STRICT DRAMA (Farce <= 1500 bp / Floor 1500 cdB)'];
  if (!dat.invariantPreset && !dat.config) {
    const invOptions = Object.keys(INVARIANT_PRESETS);
    const invRes = await promptBlessedChoice({
      promptHeader: 'Step 5/6: Configure <I> Invariant Ceilings',
      choices: invOptions,
      defaultValue: invOptions[0],
    });
    if (invRes.cancelled) {
      return abortWizard('Aborted at Step 5 (Invariant Laws <I>).');
    }
    invariantSelection = INVARIANT_PRESETS[invRes.value] || invariantSelection;
  }

  // Step 6: Central Irony Dramatic Spine via 84.input.unit
  let centralIrony = dat.centralIrony || '';
  if (!centralIrony && !dat.config) {
    const ironyRes = await promptBlessedInput({
      promptLabel: 'Step 6/6: Enter Central Irony Dramatic Spine',
      defaultValue: 'Scavengers hoard broken brass valves while starving above clean water.',
      sanitizeSlug: false,
    });
    if (ironyRes.cancelled) {
      return abortWizard('Aborted at Step 6 (Central Irony).');
    }
    centralIrony = sanitizeAscii(ironyRes.value);
  }

  const assembledConfig: AssembledFourTupleConfig = dat.config || {
    schema_version: '1.0.0',
    series_slug: seriesSlug,
    display_name: sanitizeAscii(displayName),
    genre_tag: genreTag,
    central_irony:
      centralIrony ||
      'Scavengers hoard broken brass valves while starving above clean water.',
    state_space: {
      scarcity_index_bps: stateSpaceSelection.scarcity_index_bps,
      paranoia_baseline_bps: stateSpaceSelection.paranoia_baseline_bps,
      somatic_fatigue_enabled: true,
      epistemic_ignorance_enabled: true,
      gram_mass_tiers: {
        scrap_max_grams: 50,
        weight_max_grams: 5000,
        bulk_min_grams: 5001,
      },
    },
    action_vocabulary: {
      permitted_kinetic_verbs: [...actionVocabSelection.permitted_kinetic_verbs],
      forbidden_verbs_global: [...actionVocabSelection.forbidden_verbs_global],
      locomotion_taxonomies: [...actionVocabSelection.locomotion_taxonomies],
    },
    invariant_rules: {
      max_farce_ratio_bps: invariantSelection.max_farce_ratio_bps,
      min_grievance_depth_delta: 1,
      global_acoustic_floor_cdb: invariantSelection.global_acoustic_floor_cdb,
      voiceover_ducking_db: -14,
      min_normalized_category_entropy_bps: 7000,
      thematic_categories: ['C1', 'C2', 'C3', 'C4', 'C5', 'C6', 'C7'],
    },
    medium_projections: {
      target_fps: 24,
      audio_sample_rate_hz: 48000,
      tts_service_port: 8000,
      video_service_port: 8188,
      aspect_ratio: '16:9',
    },
  };

  emitConsole(
    ste,
    `>> [GENESIS:SEAL] Dispatching 4-Tuple for '${seriesSlug}' (Genre: ${assembledConfig.genre_tag})...`,
  );

  let scaffoldBit: any = null;
  const repoRoot = dat.repoRoot || resolveRepoRoot();
  const seriesDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'series', seriesSlug);

  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    const scaffoldRes = await ste.hunt(ActSer.SCAFFOLD_SERIES_TREE, {
      idx: 'menu-genesis-scaffold',
      src: seriesSlug,
      dat: {
        seriesSlug,
        config: assembledConfig,
        repoRoot: dat.repoRoot,
      },
    });
    scaffoldBit = scaffoldRes?.seriesBit || scaffoldRes;

    if (scaffoldBit?.val === 1 || fs.existsSync(seriesDir)) {
      try {
        await ste.hunt(ActSed.POPULATE_GENESIS_SEEDS, {
          idx: 'menu-genesis-seed',
          src: seriesSlug,
          dat: {
            seriesSlug,
            repoRoot: dat.repoRoot,
          },
        });
      } catch {
        // Fallback handled by seedDirectoryExamples
      }

      seedDirectoryExamples(seriesDir, seriesSlug);
    }
  } else if (fs.existsSync(seriesDir)) {
    seedDirectoryExamples(seriesDir, seriesSlug);
  }

  const ok = !scaffoldBit || scaffoldBit.val === 1;
  const receiptLine = ok
    ? `>> [GENESIS:OK] Series created: ${seriesSlug}`
    : `:: [GENESIS:FAIL] ${scaffoldBit?.dat?.error || 'Scaffolding rejected'}`;

  emitConsole(ste, receiptLine);
  cpy.activeSelection = '[G] UNIVERSAL SERIES GENESIS WIZARD';
  cpy.lastLifecycleReceipt = ok ? '[GENESIS:OK]' : '[GENESIS:FAIL]';
  cpy.statusMessage = receiptLine;
  if (!Array.isArray(cpy.lastTelemetryReport)) {
    cpy.lastTelemetryReport = [];
  }
  cpy.lastTelemetryReport.push(receiptLine);

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: 'open-genesis-menu',
        val: ok ? 1 : 0,
        src: seriesSlug,
        dat: { receipt: receiptLine, config: assembledConfig, scaffoldBit },
      },
    });
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

#### 5. Unit Verification Suite: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/test/genesisWizard.sequential.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { MenuModel } from '../menu.model.js';
import { runSeriesGenesisMenu } from '../buz/genesisMenu.buzz.js';
import {
  promptBlessedChoice,
  promptBlessedInput,
  sanitizeSeriesSlug,
} from '../buz/blessedModal.buzz.js';

describe('299.lore - [Feature 1.1] Sequential Four-Tuple Parameterization Wizard', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    delete (globalThis as any).LIBRARY;
    delete (global as any).LIBRARY;
  });

  it('[Task 1.1.1] promptBlessedChoice anchors grid (0,4,4,8) and handles selection', async () => {
    const gridCalls: any[] = [];
    (globalThis as any).LIBRARY = {
      hunt: async (action: string, payload: any) => {
        if (action === '[Grid action] Update Grid') {
          gridCalls.push(payload);
          return { grdBit: { dat: { left: '0%', top: '33%', width: '33%', height: '66%' } } };
        }
        if (action === '[Open action] Open Choice') {
          expect(payload.net).toBeDefined();
          return { chcBit: { src: '[3] SUPERHERO URBAN VIGILANTE', val: 2 } };
        }
        return {};
      },
    };

    const res = await promptBlessedChoice({
      promptHeader: 'Select Genre',
      choices: ['[1] LOW FANTASY FARCE (Default)', '[3] SUPERHERO URBAN VIGILANTE'],
    });

    expect(res.cancelled).toBe(false);
    expect(res.value).toBe('[3] SUPERHERO URBAN VIGILANTE');
    expect(gridCalls[0]).toEqual({ x: 0, y: 4, xSpan: 4, ySpan: 8 });
  });

  it('[Task 1.1.3] promptBlessedInput captures and sanitizes series slug strings', async () => {
    (globalThis as any).LIBRARY = {
      hunt: async (action: string) => {
        if (action === '[Grid action] Update Grid') {
          return { grdBit: { dat: { left: '0%', top: '33%', width: '33%', height: '33%' } } };
        }
        if (action === '[Open action] Open Input') {
          return { putBit: { src: '  Gotham_Trinity Crisis!!  ', val: 0 } };
        }
        return {};
      },
    };

    const res = await promptBlessedInput({
      promptLabel: 'Enter Slug',
      defaultValue: 'ashfall-basin',
      sanitizeSlug: true,
    });

    expect(res.cancelled).toBe(false);
    expect(res.value).toBe('gotham-trinity-crisis');
    expect(sanitizeSeriesSlug('')).toBe('ashfall-basin');
  });

  it('[Task 1.1.2] runSeriesGenesisMenu chains 6 sequential modals and dispatches 4-Tuple to 29.series.unit', async () => {
    const tempRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'genesis-seq-'));
    const inputQueue = [
      'gotham-trinity-crisis',
      'Two heroes enforce restraint while an anarchist weaponizes chaos.',
    ];
    const choiceQueue = [
      '[3] SUPERHERO URBAN VIGILANTE',
      '[2] URBAN CRISIS (6000 bp / Paranoia 8000 bp)',
      '[2] HEROIC KINETIC (FLY, GRAPNEL, LASSO_BIND, GLIDE, DEFLECT, DETONATE)',
      '[2] NOIR ACOUSTIC (Farce <= 1000 bp / Floor 2200 cdB)',
    ];
    const dispatchedActions: Array<{ type: string; bale: any }> = [];

    (globalThis as any).LIBRARY = {
      hunt: async (action: string) => {
        if (action === '[Grid action] Update Grid') {
          return { grdBit: { dat: { left: '0%', top: '33%', width: '33%', height: '66%' } } };
        }
        if (action === '[Open action] Open Input') {
          return { putBit: { src: inputQueue.shift() || '', val: 0 } };
        }
        if (action === '[Open action] Open Choice') {
          return { chcBit: { src: choiceQueue.shift() || '', val: 0 } };
        }
        return {};
      },
    };

    const mockSte: any = {
      hunt: async (type: string, bale: any) => {
        dispatchedActions.push({ type, bale });
        return { seriesBit: { val: 1 } };
      },
    };

    const model = new MenuModel();
    let resolved: any = null;
    await runSeriesGenesisMenu(
      model,
      {
        idx: 'test-seq-wizard',
        dat: { repoRoot: tempRoot },
        slv: (res: any) => {
          resolved = res;
        },
      },
      mockSte,
    );

    expect(resolved?.mnuBit?.val).toBe(1);
    expect(resolved?.mnuBit?.src).toBe('gotham-trinity-crisis');
    const scaffoldCall = dispatchedActions.find(
      (a) => a.type === '[Series action] Scaffold Series Tree',
    );
    expect(scaffoldCall).toBeDefined();
    expect(scaffoldCall?.bale?.dat?.config?.genre_tag).toBe('SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE');
    expect(scaffoldCall?.bale?.dat?.config?.state_space?.scarcity_index_bps).toBe(6000);
    expect(
      scaffoldCall?.bale?.dat?.config?.action_vocabulary?.permitted_kinetic_verbs,
    ).toContain('FLY');
    expect(
      scaffoldCall?.bale?.dat?.config?.invariant_rules?.global_acoustic_floor_cdb,
    ).toBe(2200);

    fs.rmSync(tempRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  it('[Task 1.1.2] runSeriesGenesisMenu aborts cleanly without mutating disk when operator cancels', async () => {
    (globalThis as any).LIBRARY = {
      hunt: async (action: string) => {
        if (action === '[Open action] Open Input') {
          return { putBit: { src: '', val: -1 } };
        }
        return {};
      },
    };

    const model = new MenuModel();
    let resolved: any = null;
    await runSeriesGenesisMenu(
      model,
      {
        idx: 'test-cancel',
        slv: (res: any) => {
          resolved = res;
        },
      },
      { hunt: async () => ({}) } as any,
    );

    expect(resolved?.mnuBit?.val).toBe(0);
    expect(resolved?.mnuBit?.dat?.cancelled).toBe(true);
    expect(model.lastLifecycleReceipt).toBe('[GENESIS:CANCELLED]');
  });
});
```

### IV. CONCRETE IMPLEMENTATION SPECIFICATIONS

#### 1. Canonical Genre Registry & Zod Enum: `packages/299.lore/38.types.unit/val/genre.val.ts`

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';

export const VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS = [
  'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
  'STANDARD',
  'SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE',
  'SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL',
  'HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM',
] as const;

export const GenreClassificationEnum = z.enum(VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS);

export type GenreClassification = z.infer<typeof GenreClassificationEnum>;

export type ModalChamberFocus =
  | 'ALETHIC'
  | 'DEONTIC'
  | 'AXIOLOGICAL'
  | 'EPISTEMIC';

export interface GenreTaxonomyProfile {
  readonly id: GenreClassification;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly tone_bps: number;
  readonly physics_bps: number;
  readonly pacing_bps: number;
  readonly primary_modal_chamber: ModalChamberFocus;
  readonly secondary_modal_chamber: ModalChamberFocus;
  readonly default_max_farce_ratio_bps: number;
  readonly default_acoustic_floor_cdb: number;
}

/**
 * Integer Tri-Axial (Tone, Physics, Pacing in basis points) and Dolezel Modal
 * Chamber registry mapping each genre archetype to deterministic defaults.
 */
export const GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES: Readonly<
  Record<GenreClassification, GenreTaxonomyProfile>
> = Object.freeze({
  LOW_FANTASY_FARCE: {
    id: 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
    label: '[1] LOW FANTASY FARCE (Grim Friction / Crawlspace)',
    tone_bps: 7500,
    physics_bps: 9000,
    pacing_bps: 6500,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1500,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1500,
  },
  STANDARD: {
    id: 'STANDARD',
    label: '[2] STANDARD DRAMATIC REALISM (Grounded Baseline)',
    tone_bps: 5000,
    physics_bps: 8500,
    pacing_bps: 6000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'EPISTEMIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1000,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1800,
  },
  SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE: {
    id: 'SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE',
    label: '[3] SUPERHERO URBAN VIGILANTE (Power / Duty / Masquerade)',
    tone_bps: 4500,
    physics_bps: 6250,
    pacing_bps: 8000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'DEONTIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1000,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 2200,
  },
  SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL: {
    id: 'SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL',
    label: '[4] SOUTHERN GOTHIC INDUSTRIAL (Rot / Bloodline / Heat)',
    tone_bps: 7500,
    physics_bps: 8500,
    pacing_bps: 3500,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'DEONTIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1200,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1600,
  },
  HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM: {
    id: 'HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM',
    label: '[5] HARD SCIFI VACUUM (Orbital Mechanics / Scarcity)',
    tone_bps: 8000,
    physics_bps: 9500,
    pacing_bps: 5000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'EPISTEMIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 500,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1000,
  },
});

export function validateGenreClassification(raw: unknown): GenreClassification {
  return GenreClassificationEnum.parse(raw);
}

export function resolveGenreProfile(
  genre: GenreClassification,
): GenreTaxonomyProfile {
  return GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[genre];
}
```

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  GenreClassificationEnum,
  type GenreClassification,
} from '../../38.types.unit/val/genre.val.js';

export { GenreClassificationEnum };
export type { GenreClassification };

export const SeriesConfigSchema = z
  .object({
    $schema: z.string().optional(),
    schema_version: z.literal('1.0.0'),
    series_slug: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
    display_name: z.string().min(1),
    genre_tag: GenreClassificationEnum,
    central_irony: z.string().min(1),
    state_space: z
      .object({
        scarcity_index_bps: z.number().int().min(0).max(10000),
        paranoia_baseline_bps: z.number().int().min(0).max(10000),
        somatic_fatigue_enabled: z.boolean(),
        epistemic_ignorance_enabled: z.boolean(),
        gram_mass_tiers: z
          .object({
            scrap_max_grams: z.number().int().positive(),
            weight_max_grams: z.number().int().positive(),
            bulk_min_grams: z.number().int().positive(),
          })
          .strict(),
      })
      .strict(),
    action_vocabulary: z
      .object({
        permitted_kinetic_verbs: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
        forbidden_verbs_global: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
        locomotion_taxonomies: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
      })
      .strict(),
    invariant_rules: z
      .object({
        max_farce_ratio_bps: z.number().int().min(0).max(10000),
        min_grievance_depth_delta: z.number().int().min(1),
        global_acoustic_floor_cdb: z.number().int().min(0),
        voiceover_ducking_db: z.number().int().max(0),
        min_normalized_category_entropy_bps: z.number().int().min(0).max(10000),
        thematic_categories: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
      })
      .strict(),
    medium_projections: z
      .object({
        target_fps: z.literal(24),
        audio_sample_rate_hz: z.literal(48000),
        tts_service_port: z.number().int().positive(),
        video_service_port: z.number().int().positive(),
        aspect_ratio: z.literal('16:9'),
      })
      .strict(),
  })
  .strict();

export type SeriesConfig = z.infer<typeof SeriesConfigSchema>;
```

#### 3. Interactive Genre Modality Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/genesisMenu.buzz.ts`

Add `promptGenreModality` to `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/genesisMenu.buzz.ts` and invoke it during Step 2 of `runSeriesGenesisMenu`[cite: 49]:

```typescript
import {
  GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES,
  VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS,
  resolveGenreProfile,
  type GenreClassification,
  type GenreTaxonomyProfile,
} from '../../38.types.unit/val/genre.val.js';
import type { ModalPromptResult } from '../fce/genesisWizard.interface.js';

export const GENRE_CHOICE_MAP: Readonly<Record<string, GenreClassification>> =
  Object.freeze(
    VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS.reduce(
      (acc, genreId) => {
        const profile = GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[genreId];
        acc[profile.label] = genreId;
        return acc;
      },
      {} as Record<string, GenreClassification>,
    ),
  );

/**
 * [Task 1.2.2] Prompts the operator via 85.choice.unit (OPEN_CHOICE) to select
 * a canonical genre classification profile (including SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE
 * and STANDARD) and resolves its Tri-Axial / Modal Chamber defaults.
 */
export async function promptGenreModality(
  defaultGenre: GenreClassification = 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
): Promise<ModalPromptResult<GenreTaxonomyProfile>> {
  const defaultProfile = resolveGenreProfile(defaultGenre);
  const choices = VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS.map(
    (id) => GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[id].label,
  );

  const choiceRes = await promptBlessedChoice({
    promptHeader: 'Step 2/6: Select Genre Classification Taxonomy',
    choices,
    defaultValue: defaultProfile.label,
  });

  if (choiceRes.cancelled) {
    return {
      cancelled: true,
      value: defaultProfile,
    };
  }

  const resolvedId: GenreClassification =
    GENRE_CHOICE_MAP[choiceRes.value] || defaultGenre;

  return {
    cancelled: false,
    value: resolveGenreProfile(resolvedId),
  };
}
```

Inside `runSeriesGenesisMenu`, replace the inline genre choice call with `promptGenreModality`:

```typescript

    (dat.genreTag as GenreClassification) || 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
  );
  if (!dat.genreTag && !dat.config) {
    const genreRes = await promptGenreModality('LOW_FANTASY_FARCE');
    if (genreRes.cancelled) {
      return abortWizard('Aborted at Step 2 (Genre Classification).');
    }
    selectedGenreProfile = genreRes.value;
    emitConsole(
      ste,
      `>> [GENESIS:GENRE] Selected ${selectedGenreProfile.id} (Modal: ${selectedGenreProfile.primary_modal_chamber}/${selectedGenreProfile.secondary_modal_chamber})`,
    );
  }
  const genreTag = selectedGenreProfile.id;
```

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';

export const VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS = [
  'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
  'STANDARD',
  'SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE',
  'SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL',
  'HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM',
] as const;

export const GenreClassificationEnum = z.enum(VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS);

export type GenreClassification = z.infer<typeof GenreClassificationEnum>;

export type ModalChamberFocus =
  | 'ALETHIC'
  | 'DEONTIC'
  | 'AXIOLOGICAL'
  | 'EPISTEMIC';

export interface GenreTaxonomyProfile {
  readonly id: GenreClassification;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly tone_bps: number;
  readonly physics_bps: number;
  readonly pacing_bps: number;
  readonly primary_modal_chamber: ModalChamberFocus;
  readonly secondary_modal_chamber: ModalChamberFocus;
  readonly default_max_farce_ratio_bps: number;
  readonly default_acoustic_floor_cdb: number;
}

export const GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES: Readonly<
  Record<GenreClassification, GenreTaxonomyProfile>
> = Object.freeze({
  LOW_FANTASY_FARCE: {
    id: 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
    label: '[1] LOW FANTASY FARCE (Grim Friction / Crawlspace)',
    tone_bps: 7500,
    physics_bps: 9000,
    pacing_bps: 6500,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1500,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1500,
  },
  STANDARD: {
    id: 'STANDARD',
    label: '[2] STANDARD DRAMATIC REALISM (Grounded Baseline)',
    tone_bps: 5000,
    physics_bps: 8500,
    pacing_bps: 6000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'EPISTEMIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1000,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1800,
  },
  SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE: {
    id: 'SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE',
    label: '[3] SUPERHERO URBAN VIGILANTE (Power / Duty / Masquerade)',
    tone_bps: 4500,
    physics_bps: 6250,
    pacing_bps: 8000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'DEONTIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1000,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 2200,
  },
  SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL: {
    id: 'SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL',
    label: '[4] SOUTHERN GOTHIC INDUSTRIAL (Rot / Bloodline / Heat)',
    tone_bps: 7500,
    physics_bps: 8500,
    pacing_bps: 3500,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'DEONTIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1200,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1600,
  },
  HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM: {
    id: 'HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM',
    label: '[5] HARD SCIFI VACUUM (Orbital Mechanics / Scarcity)',
    tone_bps: 8000,
    physics_bps: 9500,
    pacing_bps: 5000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'EPISTEMIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 500,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1000,
  },
});

export function validateGenreClassification(raw: unknown): GenreClassification {
  return GenreClassificationEnum.parse(raw);
}

export function resolveGenreProfile(
  genre: GenreClassification,
): GenreTaxonomyProfile {
  return GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[genre];
}
```

#### 2. Interactive Genre Modality Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/genesisMenu.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import {
  GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES,
  VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS,
  resolveGenreProfile,
  type GenreClassification,
  type GenreTaxonomyProfile,
} from '../../38.types.unit/val/genre.val.js';
import { promptBlessedChoice } from './blessedModal.buzz.js';

export const GENRE_CHOICE_MAP: Readonly<Record<string, GenreClassification>> =
  Object.freeze(
    VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS.reduce(
      (acc, genreId) => {
        const profile = GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[genreId];
        acc[profile.label] = genreId;
        return acc;
      },
      {} as Record<string, GenreClassification>,
    ),
  );

export async function promptGenreModality(
  defaultGenre: GenreClassification = 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
): Promise<{ cancelled: boolean; value: GenreTaxonomyProfile }> {
  const defaultProfile = resolveGenreProfile(defaultGenre);
  const choices = VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS.map(
    (id) => GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[id].label,
  );

  const choiceRes = await promptBlessedChoice({
    promptHeader: 'Step 2/6: Select Genre Classification Taxonomy',
    choices,
    defaultValue: defaultProfile.label,
  });

  if (choiceRes.cancelled) {
    return { cancelled: true, value: defaultProfile };
  }

  const resolvedId: GenreClassification =
    GENRE_CHOICE_MAP[choiceRes.value] || defaultGenre;

  return { cancelled: false, value: resolveGenreProfile(resolvedId) };
}
```

### IV. CONCRETE IMPLEMENTATION SPECIFICATIONS

#### 1. Locomotion Validation Schema: `packages/299.lore/19.locomotion.unit/val/locomotion.val.ts`

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';

export const VALID_LOCOMOTION_BASELINES = [
  'bipedal_standard',
  'bipedal_hunched',
  'quadrupedal',
  'slithering',
  'brachiation',
  'sessile',
  'limping',
  'neuropathy_shuffle',
  'heavy_grounded_slog',
] as const;

export const LocomotionBaselineEnum = z.enum(VALID_LOCOMOTION_BASELINES);

export type LocomotionBaseline = z.infer<typeof LocomotionBaselineEnum>;

export interface LocomotionProfile {
  readonly baseline: LocomotionBaseline;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly max_velocity_bps: number;
  readonly default_arms_bps: number;
  readonly default_banned_verbs: readonly string[];
}

export const LOCOMOTION_PROFILES: Readonly<Record<LocomotionBaseline, LocomotionProfile>> =
  Object.freeze({
    bipedal_standard: {
      baseline: 'bipedal_standard',
      label: 'bipedal_standard (10,000 bps | 1.00x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 10000,
      default_arms_bps: 20000,
      default_banned_verbs: [],
    },
    bipedal_hunched: {
      baseline: 'bipedal_hunched',
      label: 'bipedal_hunched (8,000 bps | 0.80x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 8000,
      default_arms_bps: 20000,
      default_banned_verbs: ['sprint', 'vault'],
    },
    quadrupedal: {
      baseline: 'quadrupedal',
      label: 'quadrupedal (9,000 bps | 0.90x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 9000,
      default_arms_bps: 0,
      default_banned_verbs: ['bimanual_lift'],
    },
    slithering: {
      baseline: 'slithering',
      label: 'slithering (3,500 bps | 0.35x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 3500,
      default_arms_bps: 0,
      default_banned_verbs: ['bimanual_lift', 'grasp', 'climb'],
    },
    brachiation: {
      baseline: 'brachiation',
      label: 'brachiation (6,500 bps | 0.65x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 6500,
      default_arms_bps: 20000,
      default_banned_verbs: ['sprint'],
    },
    sessile: {
      baseline: 'sessile',
      label: 'sessile (0 bps | 0.00x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 0,
      default_arms_bps: 0,
      default_banned_verbs: ['walk', 'run', 'sprint', 'climb', 'leap'],
    },
    limping: {
      baseline: 'limping',
      label: 'limping (5,000 bps | 0.50x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 5000,
      default_arms_bps: 20000,
      default_banned_verbs: ['sprint', 'vault', 'leap', 'scramble'],
    },
    neuropathy_shuffle: {
      baseline: 'neuropathy_shuffle',
      label: 'neuropathy_shuffle (4,000 bps | 0.40x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 4000,
      default_arms_bps: 20000,
      default_banned_verbs: ['sprint', 'vault', 'leap', 'rapid_stairs', 'dive'],
    },
    heavy_grounded_slog: {
      baseline: 'heavy_grounded_slog',
      label: 'heavy_grounded_slog (4,500 bps | 0.45x)',
      max_velocity_bps: 4500,
      default_arms_bps: 20000,
      default_banned_verbs: ['leap', 'sprint'],
    },
  });

export function validateLocomotionBaseline(raw: unknown): LocomotionBaseline {
  return LocomotionBaselineEnum.parse(raw);
}

export function resolveLocomotionProfile(baseline: LocomotionBaseline): LocomotionProfile {
  return LOCOMOTION_PROFILES[baseline];
}
```

#### 2. Interactive Locomotion Menu Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/locomotionMenu.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type { MenuModel } from '../menu.model.js';
import type MenuBit from '../fce/menu.bit.js';
import {
  LOCOMOTION_PROFILES,
  VALID_LOCOMOTION_BASELINES,
  resolveLocomotionProfile,
  type LocomotionBaseline,
} from '../../19.locomotion.unit/val/locomotion.val.js';
import { resolveLibraryBus } from '../../98.menu.unit/buz/blessedModal.buzz.js';

const UPDATE_GRID = '[Grid action] Update Grid';
const OPEN_CHOICE = '[Open action] Open Choice';
const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console';

function emitConsole(ste: State | undefined, line: string): void {
  const cleanLine = String(line || '').replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '').trim();
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
    return;
  }
  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
  }
}

export const promptLocomotionChoice = async (
  cpy: MenuModel,
  bal: MenuBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<MenuModel> => {
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  const choices = VALID_LOCOMOTION_BASELINES.map((b) => LOCOMOTION_PROFILES[b].label);

  emitConsole(ste, '>> [LOCOMOTION:PROMPT] Launching baseline gait selector...');

  let selectedLabel = choices[0];
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    const gridBit: any = await lib.hunt(UPDATE_GRID, {
      x: 0,
      y: 4,
      xSpan: 4,
      ySpan: 11,
    });
    const choiceBit: any = await lib.hunt(OPEN_CHOICE, {
      dat: { clr0: 'black', clr1: 'cyan' },
      src: 'vertical',
      lst: [...choices, 'CANCEL'],
      net: gridBit?.grdBit?.dat,
    });
    selectedLabel = choiceBit?.chcBit?.src || choices[0];
  }

  if (selectedLabel === 'CANCEL' || !selectedLabel) {
    emitConsole(ste, '>> [LOCOMOTION:CANCELLED] Baseline selection aborted.');
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({ mnuBit: { idx: 'prompt-locomotion-cancel', val: 0 } });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  // Parse raw enum from label string (e.g. "bipedal_hunched (8,000 bps...)" -> "bipedal_hunched")
  const rawBaselineKey = selectedLabel.split(' ')[0].trim() as LocomotionBaseline;
  const profile = resolveLocomotionProfile(rawBaselineKey || 'bipedal_standard');

  emitConsole(ste, `>> [LOCOMOTION UNIT] Baseline selected: ${profile.baseline}`);
  emitConsole(ste, `>> [VELOCITY CAP] Max velocity: ${profile.max_velocity_bps.toLocaleString()} bps`);
  emitConsole(ste, `>> [NEGATIVE MASK] Banned: ${JSON.stringify(profile.default_banned_verbs)}`);
  if (profile.default_arms_bps === 0) {
    emitConsole(ste, '>> [ANATOMY] Upper-limb capacity clamped to 0 bps (availableArmsBps: 0).');
  }
  emitConsole(ste, '>> [STATUS] Stage T_0 Physical Bounds Locked [OK]');

  cpy.activeSelection = 'PROMPT_LOCOMOTION_CHOICE';
  cpy.statusMessage = `Locomotion locked: ${profile.baseline}`;

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: 'prompt-locomotion-success',
        val: 1,
        src: profile.baseline,
        dat: { profile },
      },
    });
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

### IV. CONCRETE IMPLEMENTATION SPECIFICATIONS

#### 1. Custom Seed Bit Contract: `packages/299.lore/27.seed.unit/fce/customSeed.bit.ts`

```typescript
export interface CustomSeedIdentifiers {
  readonly characterId?: string;
  readonly locationId?: string;
  readonly possessionId?: string;
  readonly grievanceId?: string;
}

export interface CustomSeedConfigBit {
  readonly seriesSlug: string;
  readonly customIdentifiers?: CustomSeedIdentifiers;
  readonly repoRoot?: string;
}

export interface CustomSeedResultBit {
  readonly seriesSlug: string;
  readonly injectedIds: CustomSeedIdentifiers;
  readonly totalInjected: number;
  readonly byteZeroVerified: boolean;
}

export default interface CustomSeedBit {
  readonly idx: string;
  readonly src?: string;
  readonly val?: number;
  readonly dat?: CustomSeedConfigBit;
  readonly slv?: Function;
}
```

#### 2. Custom Seed Injector Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/27.seed.unit/buz/customSeed.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import * as crypto from 'node:crypto';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type CustomSeedBit from '../fce/customSeed.bit.js';
import { resolveRepoRoot } from '../../29.series.unit/buz/scaffoldSeriesTree.buzz.js';
import { verifyByteZeroBuffer } from './seedWorldDefinitions.buzz.js';

export const injectCustomSeedIdentifiers = async (
  cpy: any,
  bal: CustomSeedBit,
  _ste?: State,
): Promise<any> => {
  const dat = bal.dat || {};
  const seriesSlug = bal.src || dat.seriesSlug;
  const customIds = dat.customIdentifiers || {};

  if (!seriesSlug) {
    if (bal.slv) {
      bal.slv({ seedBit: { idx: bal.idx, val: 0, dat: { error: 'MISSING_SERIES_SLUG' } } });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const repoRoot = dat.repoRoot || resolveRepoRoot();
  const seriesDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'series', seriesSlug);

  const charId = customIds.characterId || 'char_wonder_woman';
  const locId = customIds.locationId || 'loc_gotham_rooftop';
  const itemId = customIds.possessionId || 'item_lasso_of_truth';
  const grvId = customIds.grievanceId || 'grv_gotham_standoff';

  const charsDir = path.join(seriesDir, 'characters');
  const locsDir = path.join(seriesDir, 'locations');
  const possDir = path.join(seriesDir, 'possessions');
  const grvDir = path.join(seriesDir, 'grievances');

  for (const d of [charsDir, locsDir, possDir, grvDir]) {
    if (!fs.existsSync(d)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(d, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  const charPath = path.join(charsDir, `${charId}.md`);
  const charContent = [
    '---',
    `id: "${charId}"`,
    'title: "Wonder Woman"',
    'available_arms_bps: 20000',
    'locomotion_baseline: "bipedal_standard"',
    `held_primary: "${itemId}"`,
    'tags:',
    '  - "protagonist"',
    '  - "hero"',
    '---',
    '',
    '## Wants (surface)',
    'To preserve civilian order without compromising absolute ethical boundaries.',
    '',
    '## Wants (actual)',
    'To reconcile the chasm between divine mandate and flawed human chaos.',
    '',
  ].join('\n');
  fs.writeFileSync(charPath, charContent, { encoding: 'utf8' });

  const locPath = path.join(locsDir, `${locId}.md`);
  const locContent = [
    '---',
    `id: "${locId}"`,
    'title: "Gotham Rooftop"',
    'axial_coords:',
    '  q: 0',
    '  r: 0',
    '  s: 0',
    'max_adult_occupants: 4',
    'acoustic_damping_cdb: 1200',
    'tags:',
    '  - "location"',
    '  - "exterior"',
    '---',
    '',
    '## Overview',
    'Cold limestone gargoyles overlook the endless neon canyons of Gotham City.',
    '',
  ].join('\n');
  fs.writeFileSync(locPath, locContent, { encoding: 'utf8' });

  const itemPath = path.join(possDir, `${itemId}.md`);
  const itemContent = [
    '---',
    `id: "${itemId}"`,
    'name: "Lasso of Truth"',
    'mass_grams: 1200',
    'weight_class: "weight"',
    'slot_category: "held"',
    'hands_required_bps: 10000',
    `custody_holder: "${charId}"`,
    'tags:',
    '  - "artifact"',
    '  - "weapon"',
    '---',
    '',
    '## Description',
    'A golden cord forged from divine metallurgy that compels absolute veracity.',
    '',
  ].join('\n');
  fs.writeFileSync(itemPath, itemContent, { encoding: 'utf8' });

  const grvPath = path.join(grvDir, `${grvId}.md`);
  const grvContent = [
    '---',
    `id: "${grvId}"`,
    'title: "Gotham Standoff"',
    `participants_primary: ["${charId}"]`,
    `location_anchor: "${locId}"`,
    `physical_anchor_prop: "${itemId}"`,
    'intensity_bounds_bps: [3000, 9000]',
    'cooldown_ticks: 5',
    'tags:',
    '  - "grievance"',
    '  - "conflict"',
    '---',
    '',
    '## Surface Argument',
    'Jurisdiction and custody over the detonator amidst torrential rain.',
    '',
  ].join('\n');
  fs.writeFileSync(grvPath, grvContent, { encoding: 'utf8' });

  const allBytes = Buffer.concat([
    fs.readFileSync(charPath),
    fs.readFileSync(locPath),
    fs.readFileSync(itemPath),
    fs.readFileSync(grvPath),
  ]);
  const byteZeroVerified = verifyByteZeroBuffer(allBytes);

  const receipt = {
    seriesSlug,
    injectedIds: {
      characterId: charId,
      locationId: locId,
      possessionId: itemId,
      grievanceId: grvId,
    },
    totalInjected: 4,
    byteZeroVerified,
  };

  if (bal.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      seedBit: {
        idx: bal.idx,
        val: 1,
        dat: receipt,
      },
    });
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

#### 3. Unit Verification Suite: `packages/299.lore/27.seed.unit/test/customSeed.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { injectCustomSeedIdentifiers } from '../buz/customSeed.buzz.js';
import { SeedModel } from '../seed.model.js';

describe('299.lore - [Feature 1.5] Custom Starter Seed Identifier Injection', () => {
  let tempRepoRoot: string;

  afterEach(() => {
    if (tempRepoRoot && fs.existsSync(tempRepoRoot)) {
      fs.rmSync(tempRepoRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  it('injects Gotham-specific custom starter seed IDs while maintaining Byte-0 frontmatter', async () => {
    tempRepoRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'custom-seed-'));
    const seriesSlug = 'gotham-trinity-crisis';

    const model = new SeedModel();
    let resolved: any = null;

    const bal: any = {
      idx: 'test-custom-seed',
      src: seriesSlug,
      dat: {
        seriesSlug,
        repoRoot: tempRepoRoot,
        customIdentifiers: {
          characterId: 'char_wonder_woman',
          locationId: 'loc_gotham_rooftop',
          possessionId: 'item_lasso_of_truth',
          grievanceId: 'grv_gotham_standoff',
        },
      },
      slv: (res: any) => {
        resolved = res;
      },
    };

    await injectCustomSeedIdentifiers(model, bal);

    expect(resolved?.seedBit?.val).toBe(1);
    expect(resolved?.seedBit?.dat?.totalInjected).toBe(4);
    expect(resolved?.seedBit?.dat?.byteZeroVerified).toBe(true);

    const charFile = path.join(tempRepoRoot, 'series', seriesSlug, 'characters', 'char_wonder_woman.md');
    const locFile = path.join(tempRepoRoot, 'series', seriesSlug, 'locations', 'loc_gotham_rooftop.md');
    const itemFile = path.join(tempRepoRoot, 'series', seriesSlug, 'possessions', 'item_lasso_of_truth.md');
    const grvFile = path.join(tempRepoRoot, 'series', seriesSlug, 'grievances', 'grv_gotham_standoff.md');

    expect(fs.existsSync(charFile)).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(locFile)).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(itemFile)).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(grvFile)).toBe(true);

    // Verify Byte-0 invariant
    const charBuf = fs.readFileSync(charFile);
    expect(charBuf[0]).toBe(0x2d); // '-'
    expect(charBuf[1]).toBe(0x2d); // '-'
    expect(charBuf[2]).toBe(0x2d); // '-'
    expect(charBuf[3]).toBe(0x0a); // '\n'
  });
});
```

### I. SYSTEM LAWS & ARCHITECTURAL INVARIANTS

1. **Strict Prohibition of `packages/299.lore/src/`:**
  - Under no circumstances shall any file be created under or imported from `packages/299.lore/src/`.
  - All code must reside strictly in its designated unit directories (`29.series.unit/`, `98.menu.unit/`, etc.) registered in `packages/299.lore/BEE.ts`.
2. **Immutable Host Harness Boundary (`apps/995.library`):**
  - No files inside `apps/995.library/` may be edited or modified. Any `git diff` across `apps/995.library/995.library/` must evaluate to exactly `0` lines.
  - All Blessed curses UI interactions (`81.grid.unit`, `83.console.unit`, `84.input.unit`, `85.choice.unit`) must be driven exclusively via `(globalThis as any).LIBRARY.hunt(...)` or `(global as any).LIBRARY.hunt(...)` from `packages/299.lore`.
3. **Pure 7-Bit ASCII Compliance:**
  - Zero multi-byte UTF-8 emojis or non-ASCII characters in source code, schemas, JSON configurations, test fixtures, or console telemetry logs (`charCode <= 127`).
  - All console telemetry strings emitted to `cns00` must use pure 7-bit ASCII tokens: `>>`, `[OK]`, `[WARN]`, `[FAIL]`, `::`, `---`.
4. **Integer Basis Points Standard ($0 \le x \le 10,000\text{ bp}$):**
  - Zero IEEE-754 floating-point numbers in state spaces or schema parameters.
5. **NodeNext ESM Import Specifiers:**
  - All relative module import paths across `packages/299.lore/` must explicitly end with `.js` extensions.
6. **Title Invariant:**
  - Zero use of the word "AND" or the character "&" across all Phase, Feature, Task, and test suite titles.

### II. ARCHITECTURAL OBJECTIVE & TASK DECOMPOSITION

This directive implements **[Feature 1.1] Series Disk Discovery, Target Selection** across `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/` and `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/` to enable dynamic scanning of local disk storage for existing series namespaces (`series/` or `01.canon/*/series/`) and rendering interactive Blessed selection modals (`OPEN_CHOICE`):

- **[Task 1.1.1] Scan Series Directory:** Implement `scanVoidState` and `listSeriesSlugs` inside `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/buz/series.discovery.buzz.ts` to inspect local disk storage, validate `series.config.json` presence, and discover available series slugs.
- **[Task 1.1.2] Render Series Choice Modal:** Implement `promptSeriesSelectionModal` inside `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/seriesSelectionMenu.buzz.ts` utilizing an interactive `85.choice.unit` Blessed modal (`OPEN_CHOICE`) anchored to the 12x12 grid to let operators select their active production series context.

### III. FILE MANIFEST

1. `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/fce/seriesDiscovery.bit.ts` — Typed payload contracts for void-state and discovered series slug telemetry.
2. `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/buz/series.discovery.buzz.ts` — Filesystem scanning logic (`scanVoidState`, `listSeriesSlugs`) verifying valid `series.config.json` manifests.
3. `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/seriesSelectionMenu.buzz.ts` — Blessed TUI choice selector presenting discovered series options to the operator.
4. `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/test/seriesDiscovery.test.ts` — Colocated Vitest unit test suite verifying disk scanning, slug sorting, and empty void-state handling.

### IV. CONCRETE IMPLEMENTATION CODE

#### 1. Series Discovery Bit Contract: `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/fce/seriesDiscovery.bit.ts`

```typescript
export interface VoidScanResultBit {
  readonly isVoidState: boolean;
  readonly discoveredSlugs: readonly string[];
  readonly repoRoot: string;
  readonly seriesPath: string;
}

export default interface SeriesDiscoveryBit {
  readonly idx: string;
  readonly src?: string;
  readonly val?: number;
  readonly dat?: VoidScanResultBit;
  readonly slv?: Function;
}
```

#### 2. Series Discovery Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/buz/series.discovery.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type SeriesDiscoveryBit from '../fce/seriesDiscovery.bit.js';
import { resolveRepoRoot } from './scaffoldSeriesTree.buzz.js';
import type { VoidScanResultBit } from '../fce/seriesDiscovery.bit.js';

export const scanVoidState = (repoRootInput?: string): VoidScanResultBit => {
  const repoRoot = repoRootInput || resolveRepoRoot();
  const seriesPath = path.join(repoRoot, 'series');

  if (!fs.existsSync(seriesPath)) {
    return {
      isVoidState: true,
      discoveredSlugs: [],
      repoRoot,
      seriesPath,
    };
  }

  const entries = fs.readdirSync(seriesPath, { withFileTypes: true });
  const discoveredSlugs: string[] = [];

  for (const entry of entries) {
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      const configPath = path.join(seriesPath, entry.name, 'series.config.json');
      if (fs.existsSync(configPath)) {
        try {
          const raw = fs.readFileSync(configPath, 'utf-8');
          if (raw.trim().length > 0) {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
              discoveredSlugs.push(entry.name);
            }
          }
        } catch {
          // Ignore unparseable configs
        }
      }
    }
  }

  discoveredSlugs.sort((a, b) =>
    a === 'under-the-floorboards' ? -1 : b === 'under-the-floorboards' ? 1 : a.localeCompare(b),
  );

  return {
    isVoidState: discoveredSlugs.length === 0,
    discoveredSlugs: Object.freeze(discoveredSlugs),
    repoRoot,
    seriesPath,
  };
};

export const listDiscoveredSeriesAction = async (
  cpy: any,
  bal: SeriesDiscoveryBit,
  _ste?: State,
): Promise<any> => {
  const repoRoot = bal.src || bal.dat?.repoRoot;
  const scanResult = scanVoidState(repoRoot);

  if (bal.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      seriesBit: {
        idx: bal.idx,
        val: scanResult.isVoidState ? 0 : 1,
        dat: scanResult,
      },
    });
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

#### 3. Blessed Series Selection Menu Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/seriesSelectionMenu.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type { MenuModel } from '../menu.model.js';
import type MenuBit from '../fce/menu.bit.js';
import { scanVoidState } from '../../29.series.unit/buz/series.discovery.buzz.js';
import { resolveLibraryBus } from './blessedModal.buzz.js';

const UPDATE_GRID = '[Grid action] Update Grid';
const OPEN_CHOICE = '[Open action] Open Choice';
const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console';

function emitConsole(ste: State | undefined, line: string): void {
  const cleanLine = String(line || '').replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '').trim();
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
    return;
  }
  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
  }
}

export const promptSeriesSelectionModal = async (
  cpy: MenuModel,
  bal: MenuBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<MenuModel> => {
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  const scanResult = scanVoidState();

  if (scanResult.isVoidState) {
    emitConsole(ste, '>> [SERIES:DISCOVERY] [WARN] Void state detected: No series found in series/.');
    emitConsole(ste, '>> [SERIES:DISCOVERY] Please initialize your first series using the Genesis Wizard.');
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({ mnuBit: { idx: 'series-discovery-void', val: 0, dat: scanResult } });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const choices = scanResult.discoveredSlugs.map((slug, idx) => `[${idx + 1}] ${slug}`);
  emitConsole(ste, `>> [SERIES:DISCOVERY] Discovered ${scanResult.discoveredSlugs.length} series namespace(s) on disk.`);

  let selectedChoice = choices[0];
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    const gridBit: any = await lib.hunt(UPDATE_GRID, {
      x: 0,
      y: 4,
      xSpan: 4,
      ySpan: 10,
    });
    const choiceBit: any = await lib.hunt(OPEN_CHOICE, {
      dat: { clr0: 'black', clr1: 'yellow' },
      src: 'vertical',
      lst: [...choices, '[<] RETURN TO LORE MENU'],
      net: gridBit?.grdBit?.dat,
    });
    selectedChoice = choiceBit?.chcBit?.src || choices[0];
  }

  if (selectedChoice === '[<] RETURN TO LORE MENU' || !selectedChoice) {
    emitConsole(ste, '>> [SERIES:DISCOVERY] Series selection cancelled by operator.');
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({ mnuBit: { idx: 'series-discovery-cancel', val: 0 } });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  // Extract clean slug from "[1] under-the-floorboards"
  const cleanSlug = selectedChoice.replace(/^\[\d+\]\s*/, '').trim();
  emitConsole(ste, `>> [SERIES:ACTIVE] Bound active series namespace to: ${cleanSlug} [OK]`);

  cpy.activeSelection = 'PROMPT_SERIES_SELECTION';
  cpy.activeSeries = cleanSlug;
  cpy.statusMessage = `Active Series: ${cleanSlug}`;

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: 'series-discovery-success',
        val: 1,
        src: cleanSlug,
        dat: { scanResult, activeSlug: cleanSlug },
      },
    });
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

#### 4. Unit Verification Suite: `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/test/seriesDiscovery.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { scanVoidState } from '../buz/series.discovery.buzz.js';

describe('299.lore - [Feature 1.1] Series Disk Discovery & Target Selection', () => {
  let tempRepoRoot: string;

  afterEach(() => {
    if (tempRepoRoot && fs.existsSync(tempRepoRoot)) {
      fs.rmSync(tempRepoRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  it('detects void state when series/ directory is absent or empty', () => {
    tempRepoRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'void-test-'));
    const scan = scanVoidState(tempRepoRoot);

    expect(scan.isVoidState).toBe(true);
    expect(scan.discoveredSlugs).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('discovers valid series namespaces containing series.config.json', () => {
    tempRepoRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'discovery-test-'));
    const seriesDir = path.join(tempRepoRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis');
    fs.mkdirSync(seriesDir, { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(
      path.join(seriesDir, 'series.config.json'),
      JSON.stringify({ series_slug: 'gotham-trinity-crisis', schema_version: '1.0.0' }),
      'utf-8',
    );

    const scan = scanVoidState(tempRepoRoot);

    expect(scan.isVoidState).toBe(false);
    expect(scan.discoveredSlugs).toContain('gotham-trinity-crisis');
  });
});
```

### III. CONCRETE IMPLEMENTATION CODE

#### 1. Canonical Genre Registry & Zod Enum: `packages/299.lore/38.types.unit/val/genre.val.ts`
```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';

export const VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS = [
  'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
  'STANDARD',
  'SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE',
  'SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL',
  'HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM',
] as const;

export const GenreClassificationEnum = z.enum(VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS);

export type GenreClassification = z.infer<typeof GenreClassificationEnum>;

export type ModalChamberFocus =
  | 'ALETHIC'
  | 'DEONTIC'
  | 'AXIOLOGICAL'
  | 'EPISTEMIC';

export interface GenreTaxonomyProfile {
  readonly id: GenreClassification;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly tone_bps: number;
  readonly physics_bps: number;
  readonly pacing_bps: number;
  readonly primary_modal_chamber: ModalChamberFocus;
  readonly secondary_modal_chamber: ModalChamberFocus;
  readonly default_max_farce_ratio_bps: number;
  readonly default_acoustic_floor_cdb: number;
}

export const GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES: Readonly<
  Record<GenreClassification, GenreTaxonomyProfile>
> = Object.freeze({
  LOW_FANTASY_FARCE: {
    id: 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
    label: '[1] LOW FANTASY FARCE (Grim Friction / Crawlspace)',
    tone_bps: 7500,
    physics_bps: 9000,
    pacing_bps: 6500,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1500,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1500,
  },
  STANDARD: {
    id: 'STANDARD',
    label: '[2] STANDARD DRAMATIC REALISM (Grounded Baseline)',
    tone_bps: 5000,
    physics_bps: 8500,
    pacing_bps: 6000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'EPISTEMIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1000,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1800,
  },
  SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE: {
    id: 'SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE',
    label: '[3] SUPERHERO URBAN VIGILANTE (Power / Duty / Masquerade)',
    tone_bps: 4500,
    physics_bps: 6250,
    pacing_bps: 8000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'DEONTIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1000,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 2200,
  },
  SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL: {
    id: 'SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL',
    label: '[4] SOUTHERN GOTHIC INDUSTRIAL (Rot / Bloodline / Heat)',
    tone_bps: 7500,
    physics_bps: 8500,
    pacing_bps: 3500,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'AXIOLOGICAL',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'DEONTIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 1200,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1600,
  },
  HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM: {
    id: 'HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM',
    label: '[5] HARD SCIFI VACUUM (Orbital Mechanics / Scarcity)',
    tone_bps: 8000,
    physics_bps: 9500,
    pacing_bps: 5000,
    primary_modal_chamber: 'ALETHIC',
    secondary_modal_chamber: 'EPISTEMIC',
    default_max_farce_ratio_bps: 500,
    default_acoustic_floor_cdb: 1000,
  },
});

export function validateGenreClassification(raw: unknown): GenreClassification {
  return GenreClassificationEnum.parse(raw);
}

export function resolveGenreProfile(
  genre: GenreClassification,
): GenreTaxonomyProfile {
  return GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[genre];
}
```

#### 2. Interactive Genre Modality Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/genesisMenu.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import {
  GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES,
  VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS,
  resolveGenreProfile,
  type GenreClassification,
  type GenreTaxonomyProfile,
} from '../../38.types.unit/val/genre.val.js';
import { promptBlessedChoice } from './blessedModal.buzz.js';

export const GENRE_CHOICE_MAP: Readonly<Record<string, GenreClassification>> =
  Object.freeze(
    VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS.reduce(
      (acc, genreId) => {
        const profile = GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[genreId];
        acc[profile.label] = genreId;
        return acc;
      },
      {} as Record<string, GenreClassification>,
    ),
  );

export async function promptGenreModality(
  defaultGenre: GenreClassification = 'LOW_FANTASY_FARCE',
): Promise<{ cancelled: boolean; value: GenreTaxonomyProfile }> {
  const defaultProfile = resolveGenreProfile(defaultGenre);
  const choices = VALID_GENRE_CLASSIFICATIONS.map(
    (id) => GENRE_TAXONOMY_PROFILES[id].label,
  );

  const choiceRes = await promptBlessedChoice({
    promptHeader: 'Step 2/6: Select Genre Classification Taxonomy',
    choices,
    defaultValue: defaultProfile.label,
  });

  if (choiceRes.cancelled) {
    return { cancelled: true, value: defaultProfile };
  }

  const resolvedId: GenreClassification =
    GENRE_CHOICE_MAP[choiceRes.value] || defaultGenre;

  return { cancelled: false, value: resolveGenreProfile(resolvedId) };
}
```
