### I. SYSTEM LAWS & ARCHITECTURAL INVARIANTS

1. **Strict Prohibition of `packages/299.lore/src/`:**
  - Under no circumstances shall any file be created under or imported from `packages/299.lore/src/`.
  - All code must reside strictly inside numbered unit directories (`09.entity.unit/`, `10.escalation.unit/`, `38.types.unit/`, etc.) registered in `packages/299.lore/BEE.ts`.
2. **Immutable Host Harness Boundary (`apps/995.library`):**
  - No files inside `apps/995.library/` may be created, modified, or deleted (`0` lines modified across `git diff`).
  - All Blessed curses UI interactions must be routed through `(globalThis as any).LIBRARY.hunt(...)`.
3. **Pure 7-Bit ASCII Compliance:**
  - Zero multi-byte UTF-8 emojis or non-ASCII characters across all TypeScript source files, Zod schemas, JSON configurations, console telemetry logs, and test suites (`charCode <= 127`).
  - Console logs emitted to `cns00` must use pure 7-bit ASCII tokens: `>>`, `[OK]`, `[WARN]`, `[FAIL]`, `::`, `---`.
4. **Integer Basis Points Standard ($0 \le x \le 10,000\text{ bp}$):**
  - Zero floating-point numbers in capacity limits, limb availability vectors, or character attributes. All continuous ratios and multipliers must be stored as discrete integers in $[0, 10{,}000]$, rejecting floats with `[FLOAT_PROHIBITED]`.
5. **NodeNext ESM Import Specifiers:**
  - All relative module import paths across `packages/299.lore/` must explicitly end with `.js` extensions.
6. **Title Invariant:**
  - Zero use of the word "AND" or the character "&" across all Phase, Feature, Task, and test suite titles.

### II. ARCHITECTURAL OBJECTIVE & FILE MANIFEST

This directive implements **[Feature 2.2] Integer Basis Point Capacity, Stat Configuration** across `packages/299.lore` to enforce strict integer basis-point limits on character limb availability (`arms_available_bp`), equipment hand occupancy (`occupies_hands_bp`), and 2d6 combat statistics (Spite, Cunning, Vigor, Poise):

1. `packages/299.lore/38.types.unit/fce/capacityMetric.interface.ts` — Branded basis-point primitive and strict Zod range validations (`BasisPointSchema`).
2. `packages/299.lore/09.entity.unit/val/capacityStat.val.ts` — Zod schemas for character somatic limb capacity vectors and 2d6 personality/combat statistics.
3. `packages/299.lore/09.entity.unit/buz/capacityStatValidator.buzz.ts` — Pre-flight invariant gating logic verifying that equipped gear hand requirements do not exceed available character basis-point limits.
4. `packages/299.lore/09.entity.unit/test/capacityStat.test.ts` — Colocated Vitest unit test suite asserting integer basis-point enforcement and float rejection.

### III. CONCRETE IMPLEMENTATION CODE

#### 1. Branded Basis Point Primitives: `packages/299.lore/38.types.unit/fce/capacityMetric.interface.ts`

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';

export const BASIS_POINTS_MAX = 10000;
export const BASIS_POINTS_MIN = 0;

/**
 * Strict Basis Points Zod Schema.
 * Rejects floating-point numbers and out-of-range scalars with explicit error codes.
 */
export const BasisPointSchema = z
  .number({
    required_error: '[BASIS_POINTS_REQUIRED] Basis points value is mandatory',
    invalid_type_error: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] Basis points must be a pure integer number',
  })
  .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] Basis points must be an integer (no decimals permitted)' })
  .min(BASIS_POINTS_MIN, { message: '[OUT_OF_BOUNDS] Basis points cannot be negative (< 0)' })
  .max(BASIS_POINTS_MAX, { message: '[OUT_OF_BOUNDS] Basis points cannot exceed 10,000 (1.0)' });

export type BasisPoints = z.infer<typeof BasisPointSchema>;

export interface BasisPointMetricBit {
  readonly raw_value: number;
  readonly basis_points: BasisPoints;
  readonly normalized_ratio: number; // raw_value / 10000.0 for read-only telemetry
}

export function sanitizeBasisPoints(value: number): BasisPoints {
  if (!Number.isInteger(value)) {
    throw new TypeError(`[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] Non-integer basis points provided: ${value}`);
  }
  if (value < BASIS_POINTS_MIN || value > BASIS_POINTS_MAX) {
    throw new RangeError(`[OUT_OF_BOUNDS] Value ${value} lies outside [0, 10000] basis-point range.`);
  }
  return value as BasisPoints;
}
```

#### 2. Capacity & Stat Configuration Schemas: `packages/299.lore/09.entity.unit/val/capacityStat.val.ts`

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';
import { BasisPointSchema } from '../../38.types.unit/fce/capacityMetric.interface.js';

export const LocomotionBaselineEnum = z.enum([
  'bipedal_standard',
  'bipedal_hunched',
  'limping',
  'quadrupedal',
  'slithering',
  'aerial',
]);

export const SomaticLimbCapacitySchema = z
  .object({
    arms_available_bps: BasisPointSchema, // e.g. 20000 bp for 2 arms, 10000 bp for 1 arm
    locomotion_baseline: LocomotionBaselineEnum,
    banned_kinetic_verbs: z.array(z.string().min(1)).default([]),
  })
  .strict();

export type SomaticLimbCapacity = z.infer<typeof SomaticLimbCapacitySchema>;

export const CombatAttributeStatsSchema = z
  .object({
    spite: z.number().int().min(1).max(5).default(3),
    cunning: z.number().int().min(1).max(5).default(3),
    vigor: z.number().int().min(1).max(5).default(3),
    poise: z.number().int().min(1).max(5).default(3),
  })
  .strict();

export type CombatAttributeStats = z.infer<typeof CombatAttributeStatsSchema>;

export const EntityCapacityConfigurationSchema = z
  .object({
    entity_id: z.string().regex(/^(char|loc|item|grv)_[a-z0-9_]+$/),
    somatic_capacity: SomaticLimbCapacitySchema,
    combat_stats: CombatAttributeStatsSchema,
  })
  .strict();

export type EntityCapacityConfiguration = z.infer<typeof EntityCapacityConfigurationSchema>;
```

#### 3. Pre-Flight Capacity Gating Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/09.entity.unit/buz/capacityStatValidator.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type { SomaticLimbCapacity, CombatAttributeStats } from '../val/capacityStat.val.js';

export interface HandLoadRequirement {
  readonly item_id: string;
  readonly occupies_hands_bps: number;
}

export interface CapacityAuditReceipt {
  readonly valid: boolean;
  readonly available_arms_bps: number;
  readonly required_hands_bps: number;
  readonly deficit_bps: number;
  readonly errorCode?: string;
  readonly message?: string;
}

export const auditEntityCapacityPreFlight = (
  somatic: SomaticLimbCapacity,
  equippedItems: readonly HandLoadRequirement[] = [],
  _stats: CombatAttributeStats,
): CapacityAuditReceipt => {
  let requiredHandsBps = 0;
  for (const item of equippedItems) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(item.occupies_hands_bps)) {
      return {
        valid: false,
        available_arms_bps: somatic.arms_available_bps,
        required_hands_bps: 0,
        deficit_bps: 0,
        errorCode: 'FLOAT_PROHIBITED',
        message: `[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] Item '${item.item_id}' hand requirement must be an integer basis point.`,
      };
    }
    requiredHandsBps += item.occupies_hands_bps;
  }

  const deficitBps = requiredHandsBps - somatic.arms_available_bps;
  if (deficitBps > 0) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      available_arms_bps: somatic.arms_available_bps,
      required_hands_bps: requiredHandsBps,
      deficit_bps,
      errorCode: 'ANATOMICAL_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED',
      message: `[ANATOMICAL_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED] Required hand load (${requiredHandsBps} bp) exceeds available somatic capacity (${somatic.arms_available_bps} bp) by ${deficitBps} bp.`,
    };
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    available_arms_bps: somatic.arms_available_bps,
    required_hands_bps: requiredHandsBps,
    deficit_bps: 0,
  };
};

export const validateCapacityAction = async (
  cpy: any,
  bal: any,
  _ste?: State,
): Promise<any> => {
  const dat = bal?.dat || {};
  const audit = auditEntityCapacityPreFlight(dat.somatic, dat.equippedItems || [], dat.stats);

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      entBit: {
        idx: bal.idx,
        val: audit.valid ? 1 : 0,
        dat: audit,
      },
    });
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

#### 4. Unit Verification Suite: `packages/299.lore/09.entity.unit/test/capacityStat.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { BasisPointSchema, sanitizeBasisPoints } from '../../38.types.unit/fce/capacityMetric.interface.js';
import { auditEntityCapacityPreFlight } from '../buz/capacityStatValidator.buzz.js';
import { CombatAttributeStatsSchema } from '../val/capacityStat.val.js';

describe('299.lore - [Feature 2.2] Integer Basis Point Capacity & Stat Configuration', () => {
  it('enforces BasisPointSchema integer bounds [0, 10000] and rejects floats', () => {
    expect(BasisPointSchema.safeParse(5000).success).toBe(true);
    expect(BasisPointSchema.safeParse(0).success).toBe(true);
    expect(BasisPointSchema.safeParse(10000).success).toBe(true);

    const floatRes = BasisPointSchema.safeParse(5000.5);
    expect(floatRes.success).toBe(false);
    expect(floatRes.error?.issues[0].message).toContain('FLOAT_PROHIBITED');

    const overRes = BasisPointSchema.safeParse(10500);
    expect(overRes.success).toBe(false);
    expect(overRes.error?.issues[0].message).toContain('OUT_OF_BOUNDS');

    expect(() => sanitizeBasisPoints(5000.25)).toThrowError(/FLOAT_PROHIBITED/);
  });

  it('validates 2d6 combat statistics within [1, 5] integer bounds', () => {
    const statsValid = CombatAttributeStatsSchema.safeParse({
      spite: 4,
      cunning: 5,
      vigor: 3,
      poise: 2,
    });
    expect(statsValid.success).toBe(true);

    const statsInvalid = CombatAttributeStatsSchema.safeParse({
      spite: 6, // Out of bounds
      cunning: 2,
      vigor: 3,
      poise: 2,
    });
    expect(statsInvalid.success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('audits somatic limb capacity and flags anatomical capacity breaches', () => {
    const somatic = {
      arms_available_bps: 10000, // 1 arm available (e.g. Wonder Woman holding Lasso)
      locomotion_baseline: 'bipedal_standard' as const,
      banned_kinetic_verbs: [],
    };

    const stats = { spite: 3, cunning: 5, vigor: 4, poise: 5 };

    const validAudit = auditEntityCapacityPreFlight(
      somatic,
      [{ item_id: 'item_lasso_of_truth', occupies_hands_bps: 10000 }],
      stats,
    );
    expect(validAudit.valid).toBe(true);

    const invalidAudit = auditEntityCapacityPreFlight(
      somatic,
      [
        { item_id: 'item_lasso_of_truth', occupies_hands_bps: 10000 },
        { item_id: 'item_heavy_shield', occupies_hands_bps: 10000 },
      ],
      stats,
    );
    expect(invalidAudit.valid).toBe(false);
    expect(invalidAudit.errorCode).toBe('ANATOMICAL_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED');
  });
});
```

#### 2. Fleet Publish Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/fleetPublish.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type FleetPublishBit from '../fce/fleetPublish.bit.js';
import { resolveRepoRoot } from '../../00.lore.unit/buz/lore.buzz.js';
import { resolveLibraryBus } from './blessedModal.buzz.js';

const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console';

function emitConsole(ste: State | undefined, line: string): void {
  const cleanLine = String(line || '').replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '').trim();
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
    return;
  }
  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
  }
}

export const publishSealedCanonToFleet = async (
  cpy: any,
  bal: FleetPublishBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<any> => {
  const dat = bal.dat || {};
  const seriesSlug = bal.src || dat.seriesSlug || 'gotham-trinity-crisis';
  const repoRoot = resolveRepoRoot();

  const sourceManifest = path.resolve(repoRoot, 'compiled', seriesSlug, 'bible-state.json');

  if (!fs.existsSync(sourceManifest)) {
    const err = `[PUBLISH_ERROR] Compiled manifest not found at ${sourceManifest}. Run COMPILE LORE INSTANCE first.`;
    emitConsole(ste, `:: ${err}`);
    if (bal.slv) {
      bal.slv({ pubBit: { idx: 'publish-fleet-fail', val: -1, dat: { error: err } } });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  emitConsole(ste, `>> [FLEET:PUBLISH] Broadcasting sealed canon '${seriesSlug}' across simulation fleet...`);

  // Resolve sibling roots (assuming standard D:/trellis/ or monorepo sibling layout)
  const parentDir = path.resolve(repoRoot, '..');
  const targetEngineRoot = dat.targetEngineRoot || path.join(parentDir, '000.series-sower-engine');
  const targetSpatialRoot = dat.targetSpatialRoot || path.join(parentDir, '000.goblin-spatial');

  let engineDestination = '';
  let spatialDestination = '';

  try {
    // 1. Copy bible-state.json into 000.series-sower-engine
    if (fs.existsSync(targetEngineRoot)) {
      engineDestination = path.join(targetEngineRoot, 'compiled', seriesSlug, 'bible-state.json');
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(engineDestination), { recursive: true });
      fs.copyFileSync(sourceManifest, engineDestination);
      emitConsole(ste, `>> [ENGINE:SYNC] [OK] Staged canon into 000.series-sower-engine`);
    } else {
      emitConsole(ste, `>> [ENGINE:SYNC] [WARN] Sibling repo 000.series-sower-engine not found at ${targetEngineRoot}`);
    }

    // 2. Export topology manifest into 000.goblin-spatial
    if (fs.existsSync(targetSpatialRoot)) {
      spatialDestination = path.join(targetSpatialRoot, 'manifests', `${seriesSlug}-topology.json`);
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(spatialDestination), { recursive: true });
      fs.copyFileSync(sourceManifest, spatialDestination);
      emitConsole(ste, `>> [SPATIAL:SYNC] [OK] Exported room topology manifest into 000.goblin-spatial`);
    } else {
      emitConsole(ste, `>> [SPATIAL:SYNC] [WARN] Sibling repo 000.goblin-spatial not found at ${targetSpatialRoot}`);
    }

    emitConsole(ste, `>> [FLEET:PUBLISH] [OK] Fleet broadcast successfully sealed and completed.`);

    if (bal.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        pubBit: {
          idx: 'publish-fleet-success',
          val: 1,
          src: seriesSlug,
          dat: {
            published: true,
            seriesSlug,
            engineDestination,
            spatialDestination,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
          },
        },
      });
    }
  } catch (err: any) {
    const errorMsg = err.message || String(err);
    emitConsole(ste, `:: [PUBLISH_FAULT] Fleet publication failed: ${errorMsg}`);
    if (bal.slv) {
      bal.slv({ pubBit: { idx: 'publish-fleet-error', val: -1, dat: { error: errorMsg } } });
    }
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { publishSealedCanonToFleet } from '../buz/fleetPublish.buzz.js';

describe('299.lore - [Feature 1.1] Goblin Lore Fleet Publishing Menu Integration', () => {
  let tempFleetRoot: string;

  afterEach(() => {
    if (tempFleetRoot && fs.existsSync(tempFleetRoot)) {
      fs.rmSync(tempFleetRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  it('automatically copies compiled bible-state.json into target engine and spatial sibling roots', async () => {
    tempFleetRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'fleet-pub-'));
    const loreRoot = path.join(tempFleetRoot, '000.goblin-lore');
    const engineRoot = path.join(tempFleetRoot, '000.series-sower-engine');
    const spatialRoot = path.join(tempFleetRoot, '000.goblin-spatial');

    const seriesSlug = 'gotham-trinity-crisis';
    const compiledSource = path.join(loreRoot, 'compiled', seriesSlug, 'bible-state.json');
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(compiledSource), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(compiledSource, JSON.stringify({ series_slug: seriesSlug, _meta: { state_hash: 'abc123hash' } }), 'utf-8');

    let resolved: any = null;
    const bal: any = {
      idx: 'test-fleet-pub',
      src: seriesSlug,
      dat: {
        seriesSlug,
        targetEngineRoot: engineRoot,
        targetSpatialRoot: spatialRoot,
      },
      slv: (res: any) => {
        resolved = res;
      },
    };

    // Mock resolveRepoRoot to point to temp lore root
    vi.mock('../../00.lore.unit/buz/lore.buzz.js', () => ({
      resolveRepoRoot: () => loreRoot,
    }));

    await publishSealedCanonToFleet({}, bal, { hunt: async () => ({}) } as any);

    expect(resolved?.pubBit?.val).toBe(1);
    expect(resolved?.pubBit?.dat?.published).toBe(true);

    const targetEngineFile = path.join(engineRoot, 'compiled', seriesSlug, 'bible-state.json');
    const targetSpatialFile = path.join(spatialRoot, 'manifests', `${seriesSlug}-topology.json`);

    expect(fs.existsSync(targetEngineFile)).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(targetSpatialFile)).toBe(true);

    const engineContent = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(targetEngineFile, 'utf-8'));
    expect(engineContent.series_slug).toBe(seriesSlug);
  });
});
```

```typescript
import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type TopologyExportBit from '../fce/topologyExport.bit.js';
import { resolveRepoRoot } from '../../00.lore.unit/buz/lore.buzz.js';
import { resolveLibraryBus } from './blessedModal.buzz.js';

const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console';

function emitConsole(ste: State | undefined, line: string): void {
  const cleanLine = String(line || '').replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '').trim();
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
    return;
  }
  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
  }
}

export const exportStaticTopologyManifest = async (
  cpy: any,
  bal: TopologyExportBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<any> => {
  const dat = bal.dat || {};
  const seriesSlug = bal.src || dat.seriesSlug || 'gotham-trinity-crisis';
  const repoRoot = resolveRepoRoot();

  const compiledBiblePath = path.resolve(repoRoot, 'compiled', seriesSlug, 'bible-state.json');

  if (!fs.existsSync(compiledBiblePath)) {
    const err = `[TOPOLOGY_EXPORT_ERROR] Compiled bible-state.json not found at ${compiledBiblePath}.`;
    emitConsole(ste, `:: ${err}`);
    if (bal.slv) {
      bal.slv({ topBit: { idx: 'export-topology-fail', val: -1, dat: { error: err } } });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  emitConsole(ste, `>> [TOPOLOGY:EXPORT] Extracting static room geometry manifest for '${seriesSlug}'...`);

  try {
    const bibleContent = fs.readFileSync(compiledBiblePath, 'utf-8');
    const bibleState = JSON.parse(bibleContent);
    const locations = bibleState?.entities?.locations || {};

    const spatialNodes: Record<string, any> = {};
    let nodeCount = 0;

    for (const [locId, locData] of Object.entries<any>(locations)) {
      spatialNodes[locId] = {
        locationId: locId,
        displayName: locData.title || locId,
        adjacentLocations: locData.adjacent_locations || [],
        acousticDampingFactor: locData.acoustic_damping_cdb || 1500,
        lightingLevel: locData.lighting_level || 'INCANDESCENT',
        volumeCapacity: {
          maxAdultGoblins: locData.max_adult_goblins || 2,
          isCramped: Boolean(locData.is_cramped),
        },
      };
      nodeCount++;
    }

    const topologyManifest = {
      schema_version: '1.0.0',
      series_slug: seriesSlug,
      merkle_root: bibleState?._meta?.state_hash || '0'.repeat(64),
      nodes: spatialNodes,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const parentDir = path.resolve(repoRoot, '..');
    const targetSpatialRoot = dat.targetSpatialRoot || path.join(parentDir, '000.goblin-spatial');

    if (!fs.existsSync(targetSpatialRoot)) {
      emitConsole(ste, `>> [TOPOLOGY:WARN] Sibling repository 000.goblin-spatial not found at ${targetSpatialRoot}`);
      if (bal.slv) {
        bal.slv({
          topBit: {
            idx: 'export-topology-no-spatial',
            val: 0,
            dat: { topologyManifest, nodeCount },
          },
        });
      }
      return cpy;
    }

    const spatialDestination = path.join(targetSpatialRoot, 'manifests', `${seriesSlug}-topology.json`);
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(spatialDestination), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(spatialDestination, JSON.stringify(topologyManifest, null, 2), 'utf-8');

    emitConsole(ste, `>> [TOPOLOGY:EXPORT] [OK] Exported ${nodeCount} node(s) into 000.goblin-spatial/manifests/${seriesSlug}-topology.json`);

    if (bal.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        topBit: {
          idx: 'export-topology-success',
          val: 1,
          src: seriesSlug,
          dat: {
            exported: true,
            seriesSlug,
            spatialDestination,
            nodeCount,
            timestamp: topologyManifest.timestamp,
          },
        },
      });
    }
  } catch (err: any) {
    const errorMsg = err.message || String(err);
    emitConsole(ste, `:: [TOPOLOGY_FAULT] Topology export failed: ${errorMsg}`);
    if (bal.slv) {
      bal.slv({ topBit: { idx: 'export-topology-error', val: -1, dat: { error: errorMsg } } });
    }
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { exportStaticTopologyManifest } from '../buz/topologyExport.buzz.js';

describe('299.lore - [Task 1.1.3] Route Static Topology Export', () => {
  let tempFleetRoot: string;

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
    if (tempFleetRoot && fs.existsSync(tempFleetRoot)) {
      fs.rmSync(tempFleetRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  it('extracts room geometry manifests and pushes TopologyManifest into 000.goblin-spatial', async () => {
    tempFleetRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'topology-exp-'));
    const loreRoot = path.join(tempFleetRoot, '000.goblin-lore');
    const spatialRoot = path.join(tempFleetRoot, '000.goblin-spatial');

    const seriesSlug = 'gotham-trinity-crisis';
    const compiledBiblePath = path.join(loreRoot, 'compiled', seriesSlug, 'bible-state.json');
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(compiledBiblePath), { recursive: true });

    const mockBibleState = {
      _meta: { state_hash: 'aabbccdd11223344' },
      entities: {
        locations: {
          loc_gotham_rooftop: {
            title: 'Gotham Rooftop',
            adjacent_locations: ['loc_gotham_chemical_plant'],
            acoustic_damping_cdb: 2200,
            lighting_level: 'INCANDESCENT',
            max_adult_goblins: 4,
            is_cramped: false,
          },
        },
      },
    };
    fs.writeFileSync(compiledBiblePath, JSON.stringify(mockBibleState), 'utf-8');

    let resolved: any = null;
    const bal: any = {
      idx: 'test-topology-export',
      src: seriesSlug,
      dat: {
        seriesSlug,
        targetSpatialRoot: spatialRoot,
      },
      slv: (res: any) => {
        resolved = res;
      },
    };

    vi.mock('../../00.lore.unit/buz/lore.buzz.js', () => ({
      resolveRepoRoot: () => loreRoot,
    }));

    await exportStaticTopologyManifest({}, bal, { hunt: async () => ({}) } as any);

    expect(resolved?.topBit?.val).toBe(1);
    expect(resolved?.topBit?.dat?.exported).toBe(true);
    expect(resolved?.topBit?.dat?.nodeCount).toBe(1);

    const targetTopologyFile = path.join(spatialRoot, 'manifests', `${seriesSlug}-topology.json`);
    expect(fs.existsSync(targetTopologyFile)).toBe(true);

    const exportedManifest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(targetTopologyFile, 'utf-8'));
    expect(exportedManifest.series_slug).toBe(seriesSlug);
    expect(exportedManifest.nodes.loc_gotham_rooftop.acousticDampingFactor).toBe(2200);
  });
});
```

```typescript
import type { Action } from '../99.core/interface/action.interface.js';
import type MenuBit from './fce/menu.bit.js';

export const EXPORT_SIGNED_SPATIAL_RECEIPT = '[Menu action] Export Signed Spatial Receipt';
export class ExportSignedSpatialReceipt implements Action<MenuBit> {
  readonly type = EXPORT_SIGNED_SPATIAL_RECEIPT;
  constructor(public bale?: MenuBit) {}
}

export type Actions =
  | InitMenu
  | UpdateMenu
  | ExecuteBoundaryVerification
  | RunOracleReciprocityAudit
  | TestWorkBudgetTripwires
  | ExportSignedSpatialReceipt;
```

#### 2. Reducer Router: `packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit/menu.reduce.ts`

```typescript
import clone from 'clone-deep';
import * as Act from './menu.action.js';
import { MenuModel } from './menu.model.js';
import * as Buzz from './menu.buzzer.js';
import type State from '../99.core/state.js';

export function reducer(
  model: MenuModel = new MenuModel(),
  act: Act.Actions,
  state?: State
) {
  switch (act.type) {
    case Act.INIT_MENU:
      return Buzz.initMenu(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.UPDATE_MENU:
      return Buzz.updateMenu(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.EXPORT_SIGNED_SPATIAL_RECEIPT:
      return Buzz.exportSignedSpatialReceiptMenuBuzz(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    default:
      return model;
  }
}
```

#### 3. Menu Buzzer Integration: `packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit/buz/00.menu.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type { MenuModel } from '../menu.model.js';
import type MenuBit from '../fce/menu.bit.js';
import * as ActCns from '../../83.console.unit/console.action.js';

const WRITE_CONSOLE = '[Write action] Write Console';

export const EXPORT_RECEIPT_MENU_LABEL = '[P] EXPORT SIGNED SPATIAL RECEIPT TO ENGINE';

export const exportSignedSpatialReceiptMenuBuzz = async (
  cpy: MenuModel,
  bal: MenuBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<MenuModel> => {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY;

  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    await lib.hunt(WRITE_CONSOLE, {
      idx: 'cns00',
      src: '>> [SPATIAL::EXPORT] Packaging cryptographic spatial receipts for 000.series-sower-engine...',
    });
    await lib.hunt(WRITE_CONSOLE, {
      idx: 'cns00',
      src: '>> [SPATIAL::EXPORT] [OK] Raycast and acoustic proofs staged successfully.',
    });
  }

  cpy.lastActionTimestamp = Date.now();
  cpy.statusMessage = 'SPATIAL_RECEIPT_EXPORTED';

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: 'export-spatial-receipt-success',
        val: 1,
        src: 'gotham-trinity-crisis',
      },
    });
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

#### 1. Sensory Evidence & Percept Receipt Contracts: `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/fce/receipt.interface.ts`

```typescript
export type SensoryModality = 'OPTICAL' | 'ACOUSTIC';

export interface AxialCoord {
  readonly q: number;
  readonly r: number;
  readonly s: number;
}

export interface OpticalPerceptEvidence {
  readonly modality: 'OPTICAL';
  readonly transmissionBps: number; // [0, 10000] basis points
  readonly pathLengthAxial: number;
  readonly occludingBarrierCoord?: AxialCoord;
}

export interface AcousticPerceptEvidence {
  readonly modality: 'ACOUSTIC';
  readonly intensityCdb: number; // centi-decibels (1 dB = 100 cdB)
  readonly noiseFloorCdb: number;
  readonly snrMarginCdb: number;
  readonly portalHopCount: number;
}

export type PerceptEvidence = OpticalPerceptEvidence | AcousticPerceptEvidence;

export interface AuditQuadruple {
  readonly topologyHash: string;
  readonly overlayHash: string;
  readonly queryHash: string;
  readonly kernelVersion: string;
}

export interface PerceptCandidate {
  readonly sourceCoord: AxialCoord;
  readonly observerCoord: AxialCoord;
  readonly qualityScorePerMille: number; // [0, 1000]
  readonly arrivalBearing: number | null; // 0..5 axial direction or null if collocated
  readonly isOccluded: boolean;
  readonly evidence: PerceptEvidence;
  readonly auditQuadruple: AuditQuadruple;
}
```

#### 2. Receipt Minting Buzzer: `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/buz/receipt.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import type { PerceptCandidate, PerceptEvidence, AuditQuadruple, AxialCoord } from '../fce/receipt.interface.js';

export interface MintPerceptParams {
  readonly sourceCoord: AxialCoord;
  readonly observerCoord: AxialCoord;
  readonly qualityScorePerMille: number;
  readonly arrivalBearing: number | null;
  readonly isOccluded: boolean;
  readonly evidence: PerceptEvidence;
  readonly auditQuadruple: AuditQuadruple;
}

export const mintPerceptReceipt = (model: any, bal: any): any => {
  const params: MintPerceptParams = bal?.dat?.perceptCandidate || bal?.perceptCandidate;

  if (!params || !params.evidence || !params.auditQuadruple) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({ rcpBit: { idx: 'mint-receipt-err', val: -1, dat: { error: 'INVALID_PERCEPT_PARAMS' } } });
    }
    return model;
  }

  const candidate: PerceptCandidate = Object.freeze({
    sourceCoord: Object.freeze({ ...params.sourceCoord }),
    observerCoord: Object.freeze({ ...params.observerCoord }),
    qualityScorePerMille: params.qualityScorePerMille,
    arrivalBearing: params.arrivalBearing,
    isOccluded: params.isOccluded,
    evidence: Object.freeze({ ...params.evidence }),
    auditQuadruple: Object.freeze({ ...params.auditQuadruple }),
  });

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      rcpBit: {
        idx: 'mint-receipt-success',
        val: 1,
        dat: { perceptCandidate: candidate },
      },
    });
  }

  return model;
};
```

#### 3. Unit Verification Suite: `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/test/receipt.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { mintPerceptReceipt } from '../buz/receipt.buzz.js';
import type { PerceptCandidate } from '../fce/receipt.interface.js';

describe('301.spatial - [Task 1.2.2] Package Acoustic & Optical Raycast Proofs', () => {
  const AUDIT_QUADRUPLE = {
    topologyHash: 'topo_abc123',
    overlayHash: 'overlay_def456',
    queryHash: 'query_789xyz',
    kernelVersion: '0.1.4',
  };

  it('aggregates acoustic wavefront proof into a sealed, frozen PerceptCandidate receipt', () => {
    let captured: PerceptCandidate | null = null;
    const bale = {
      idx: 'receipt-audit-acoustic',
      dat: {
        perceptCandidate: {
          sourceCoord: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
          observerCoord: { q: 0, r: 3, s: -3 },
          qualityScorePerMille: 700,
          arrivalBearing: 3,
          isOccluded: false,
          evidence: {
            modality: 'ACOUSTIC',
            intensityCdb: 5000,
            noiseFloorCdb: 2700,
            snrMarginCdb: 2300,
            portalHopCount: 2,
          },
          auditQuadruple: AUDIT_QUADRUPLE,
        },
      },
      slv: (res: any) => {
        captured = res.rcpBit.dat?.perceptCandidate ?? null;
      },
    };

    mintPerceptReceipt({}, bale);

    expect(captured).not.toBeNull();
    expect(captured?.evidence.modality).toBe('ACOUSTIC');
    if (captured?.evidence.modality === 'ACOUSTIC') {
      expect(captured.evidence.snrMarginCdb).toBe(2300);
      expect(captured.evidence.intensityCdb).toBe(5000);
    }
    expect(Object.isFrozen(captured)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### **User Story**

As an operator launching the sovereign simulation kernel (`000.series-sower-engine`) via `npm run dev`, I want the Blessed Terminal Interface runner (`apps/995.library/run.ts`) to seamlessly bind `300.engine` so that executing `[01] BOOT ENGINE & ENSURE SCHEMA` initializes local SQLite storage, seeds primordial genesis ($S_0$), and dynamically transitions the left control panel into the runtime dispatch menu (`[14] EXECUTE DETERMINISTIC`, `ADVANCE TICK`, and `RUN SIM GAUNTLET`) without breaking harness immutability.

#### **Strategic Goal**

Supports the goal of deterministic, zero-latency local simulation flight deck orchestration at $T_0 = \$0.00$.

#### **Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Establishes the primary runtime bridge between the Blessed terminal harness and the pure simulation kernel, enabling operators to drive storyworld execution and invariant audits interactively.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Wiring the dynamic package route switchboard in `apps/995.library/run.ts` while maintaining a strict zero-edit quarantine on `apps/995.library/995.library/**` and enforcing pure 7-bit ASCII compliance.

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### **User Story**

The showrunner stared at the cold terminal screen, preparing to forge a new narrative universe—one where high-altitude urban vigilantes patrolled rain-slicked rooftops rather than goblins scavenging beneath floorboards. Previously, every new series was locked into a single, rigid crawlspace farce template, forcing worldbuilders to manually rewrite configuration schemas before a single character could take a breath. With **Genre Classification Taxonomy Expansion**, the operator opens the Blessed terminal flight deck, steps into the Universal Series Genesis Wizard, and selects from an expanded palette of Tri-Axial genre archetypes—such as `SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE` or `STANDARD`. In a single keystroke, the compiler calibrates the universe's environmental tone, sensory physics, temporal pacing, and Lubomír Doležel modal chamber constraints (`ALETHIC`, `DEONTIC`, `AXIOLOGICAL`, `EPISTEMIC`), laying down an incorruptible legislative foundation at $T_0 = \$0.00$.

#### **Strategic Goal**

Supports the core architectural goal of Meta-Ontology vs. Story Instance decoupling ($\langle \mathcal{S}, \mathcal{A}, \mathcal{I}, \mathcal{R}_m \rangle$), enabling `000.goblin-lore` to parameterize diverse multi-genre storyworlds at $T_0 = \$0.00$ without hardcoding single-franchise assumptions or risking runtime schema drift.

#### **Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Decouples the universal narrative compiler from single-genre goblin crawlspace defaults, allowing downstream simulation engines and spatial oracles to inherit genre-calibrated basis-point caps, acoustic floors, and modal chamber postures automatically from `series.config.json`.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Expanding `SeriesConfigSchema` and the Blessed TUI choice modals to support multi-genre Tri-Axial presets (`tone_bps`, `physics_bps`, `pacing_bps`) while enforcing strict integer basis points ($0 \le x \le 10{,}000\text{ bp}$), pure 7-bit ASCII compliance, and NodeNext `.js` module resolution without touching the immutable `apps/995.library/` harness.

#### **Interface**

- **`000.goblin-lore` (`packages/299.lore`):** Coordinates internal unit contracts across `38.types.unit/val/genre.val.ts` (Tri-Axial & Modal Chamber registry), `29.series.unit/fce/series.interface.ts` (`SeriesConfigSchema`), and `98.menu.unit/buz/genesisMenu.buzz.ts` (`promptGenreModality`).
- **`000.series-sower-engine` (`packages/300.engine`):** Consumes the compiled `bible-state.json` generated from the expanded `series.config.json` genre taxonomy to govern simulation tension clocks, farce ceilings, and dialogue curtness budgets.
- **`000.goblin-spatial` (`packages/301.spatial`):** Inherits the genre-calibrated `default_acoustic_floor_cdb` and physical constraint parameters exported from the sealed lore manifest.
- **`000.series-sower-conductor` (`packages/400.conductor`):** Consumes downstream AST and exposure sheet projections parameterized by the series genre classification.

#### **Interaction**

- **Blessed Terminal Interface (TUI):** Launch the 12x12 dual-pane Blessed curses cockpit via `npm run dev` in `000.goblin-lore`, navigate the left control pane to **LORE MENU**, select **[G] UNIVERSAL SERIES GENESIS WIZARD**, enter a series slug, and use the `Up`/`Down` arrow keys and `Enter` in the **Step 2/6: Select Genre Classification Taxonomy** `OPEN_CHOICE` modal to select `SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE` or `STANDARD` while observing real-time 7-bit ASCII telemetry (`>> [GENESIS:GENRE]`) in the right-hand `cns00` console pane.

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### **User Story**

The showrunner stared at the Series Genesis Wizard prompt in `000.goblin-lore`, ready to launch a high-altitude urban vigilante saga across Gotham rooftops rather than a subterranean crawlspace farce. Previously, every new series defaulted to a static crawler verb vocabulary that hard-coded `"fly"` into `forbidden_verbs_global`, immediately causing the compiler to reject heroic aerial traversal and signature grappling maneuvers at $T_0 = \$0.00$. With **Kinetic Verb Vocabulary Customization**, the operator selects a heroic kinetic preset—or enters a custom comma-separated allowlist such as `FLY, GRAPNEL, LASSO_BIND, GLIDE, DEFLECT, DETONATE`—directly inside the Blessed terminal cockpit. In one deterministic pass, the ontology engine normalizes the tokens into uppercase ASCII, registers them across closed semantic verb families, strips `"fly"` from `forbidden_verbs_global`, and aligns locomotion taxonomies so the narrative universe compiles cleanly without schema contradiction.

#### **Strategic Goal**

Supports the core architectural goal of Meta-Ontology vs. Story Instance decoupling ($\langle \mathcal{S}, \mathcal{A}, \mathcal{I}, \mathcal{R}_m \rangle$) and deterministic $T_0 = \$0.00$ somatic enforcement, enabling `000.goblin-lore` to parameterize custom kinetic action vocabularies and global ban-list overrides per series without runtime schema drift or LLM evasion.

#### **Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Replaces static, single-genre verb blacklists with an extensible, series-scoped kinetic verb ontology (`39.verbs.unit`), empowering worldbuilders to authorize specialized physical capabilities (`FLY`, `GRAPNEL`, `LASSO_BIND`, `GLIDE`, `DEFLECT`, `DETONATE`) while preserving strict $O(1)$ set-intersection gating against genuinely prohibited actions.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Expanding `KineticVerbEnum` and `VERB_FAMILIES` (`LOCOMOTION_AERIAL`, `HEROIC_TACTICAL`) while ensuring `customizeKineticVocabulary` deterministically reconciles `forbidden_verbs_global` (stripping `"fly"` whenever `FLY` is permitted) and adheres to pure 7-bit ASCII, NodeNext `.js` import specifiers, and the immutable `apps/995.library/` harness boundary.

#### **Interface**

- **`000.goblin-lore` (`packages/299.lore`):** Coordinates internal unit contracts across `39.verbs.unit/fce/verbs.schema.ts` (`KineticVerbEnum`, `VERB_FAMILIES`), `39.verbs.unit/val/verbs.val.ts` (`KineticVerbLemmaSchema`), `39.verbs.unit/buz/verbs.buzz.ts` (`customizeKineticVocabulary`), and `98.menu.unit/buz/genesisMenu.buzz.ts` (`promptKineticVocabularyMenu`).
- **`000.series-sower-engine` (`packages/300.engine`):** Consumes the compiled `bible-state.json` action vocabulary and reconciled `forbidden_verbs_global` list to enforce Stage 5 dual-field dialogue AST and somatic panopticon checks.
- **`000.goblin-spatial` (`packages/301.spatial`):** Aligns spatial traversal and aerial/grapnel movement feasibility checks against the series' authorized kinetic verb and locomotion taxonomy.

#### **Interaction**

- **Blessed Terminal Interface (TUI):** Launch the 12x12 dual-pane Blessed curses cockpit via `npm run dev` in `000.goblin-lore`, navigate the left control pane to **LORE MENU**, select **[G] UNIVERSAL SERIES GENESIS WIZARD**, advance to **Step 4/6: Customize Bounded Kinetic Verb Vocabulary `<A>`**, and use the `Up`/`Down` arrow keys and `Enter` in the `OPEN_CHOICE` modal (or enter comma-separated verbs in the `OPEN_INPUT` modal) while observing real-time 7-bit ASCII telemetry in the right-hand `cns00` console pane.

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### **User Story**

The showrunner peered into the Blessed terminal flight deck of `000.goblin-lore`, configuring the physical boundaries of a new urban vigilante universe where heroes scale rain-slicked rooftops while injured villains limp through flooded alleyways. Previously, every actor in the simulation defaulted to a single uniform movement speed, allowing characters with crushed legs to sprint across rooms or aerial vigilantes to remain grounded without proper kinetic constraints. With **[Feature 1.4] Somatic Locomotion Profile Configuration**, the worldbuilder invokes an interactive gait selector directly inside the terminal cockpit, choosing from nine canonical locomotion baselines (`bipedal_standard`, `bipedal_hunched`, `limping`, `neuropathy_shuffle`, `quadrupedal`, `slithering`, `sessile`, `brachiation`, and `heavy_grounded_slog`). In a single keystroke, the compiler locks the actor's integer basis-point velocity ceiling, clamps upper-limb capacity to zero for non-articulated gaits, and automatically injects mandatory forbidden kinetic verbs into the negative motor mask at $T_0 = \$0.00$.

#### **Strategic Goal**

Supports the core architectural goal of deterministic $T_0 = \$0.00$ somatic constraint enforcement and Gillian integer basis-point purity ($0 \le x \le 10{,}000\text{ bp}$), ensuring physical gait limitations are mathematically locked before downstream simulation or generative rendering occurs.

#### **Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Replaces subjective prose movement descriptions with deterministic, integer-scaled velocity caps (`max_velocity_bps`) and automated feature roll-up (`default_banned_verbs`), preventing downstream simulation engines and language models from generating physically impossible character actions.

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### **User Story**

The showrunner peered into the Blessed terminal flight deck of `000.goblin-lore`, ready to switch production focus between subterranean crawlspaces and high-altitude urban vigilante crises across Gotham rooftops. Previously, operators had to rely on hardcoded target slugs or blind file paths, risking compiling or mutating the wrong story universe when multiple series existed on disk. With **[Feature 1.1] Series Disk Discovery, Target Selection**, the operator triggers the interactive series selector inside the 12x12 Blessed curses cockpit. Beneath the surface, `scanVoidState` dynamically crawls `series/` on disk, validates every `series.config.json` manifest, and mounts an interactive `85.choice.unit` (`OPEN_CHOICE`) modal in the left control pane (`x: 0, y: 4, xSpan: 4, ySpan: 10`), allowing the showrunner to lock their active production namespace in a single keystroke at $T_0 = \$0.00$.

#### **Strategic Goal**

Supports the core architectural goal of **Zero-Spend Cold-Boot Sentinel Protection & Deterministic Multi-Series Namespace Isolation ($T_0 = \$0.00$)**, ensuring `000.goblin-lore` dynamically discovers valid storyworlds on local disk and prevents ungrounded execution against missing or wrong series contexts.

#### **Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Replaces static, hardcoded series assumptions with dynamic filesystem discovery (`scanVoidState`) and interactive operator target binding (`promptSeriesSelectionModal`), eliminating cross-universe contamination and cold-boot crash loops.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Implementing synchronous directory crawling and JSON manifest validation inside `packages/299.lore/29.series.unit/` while driving an interactive `85.choice.unit` (`OPEN_CHOICE`) Blessed modal in `98.menu.unit/` under pure 7-bit ASCII cleanliness, NodeNext `.js` import specifiers, and zero edits to the immutable `apps/995.library/` harness.

#### **Interface**

- **`000.goblin-lore` (`packages/299.lore`):** Coordinates internal unit contracts across `29.series.unit/fce/seriesDiscovery.bit.ts` (`VoidScanResultBit`, `SeriesDiscoveryBit`), `29.series.unit/buz/series.discovery.buzz.ts` (`scanVoidState`, `listDiscoveredSeriesAction`), and `98.menu.unit/buz/seriesSelectionMenu.buzz.ts` (`promptSeriesSelectionModal`).
- **`000.series-sower-engine` (`packages/300.engine`):** Consumes the active selected series slug to resolve isolated SQLite storage (`data/sqlite/<series-slug>.local.db`) and mount the corresponding `compiled/<slug>/bible-state.json`.
- **`000.goblin-spatial` (`packages/301.spatial`):** Targets the selected series namespace when loading exported room topology manifests (`manifests/<slug>-topology.json`).

#### **Interaction**

- **Blessed Terminal Interface (TUI):** Launch the 12x12 dual-pane Blessed curses cockpit via `npm run dev` in `000.goblin-lore`, navigate the left control pane to **LORE MENU**, trigger the series discovery selector (`promptSeriesSelectionModal`), use the `Up`/`Down` arrow keys and `Enter` in the `OPEN_CHOICE` modal (`x: 0, y: 4, xSpan: 4, ySpan: 10`) to select a discovered series slug (e.g., `gotham-trinity-crisis` or `under-the-floorboards`), and observe real-time 7-bit ASCII telemetry (`>> [SERIES:DISCOVERY]`, `>> [SERIES:ACTIVE]`) streaming into the right-hand `cns00` console pane.

### IV. CONCRETE IMPLEMENTATION CODE

#### 1. Domain Entity Selector Bit Contract: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/fce/domainSelector.bit.ts`

```typescript
export type DomainEntityType =
  | 'character'
  | 'location'
  | 'possession'
  | 'grievance'
  | 'world';

export interface DomainEntityDescriptor {
  readonly domain: DomainEntityType;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly idPrefix: 'char_' | 'loc_' | 'item_' | 'grv_' | 'law_';
  readonly directoryName: 'characters' | 'locations' | 'possessions' | 'grievances' | 'world';
}

export const DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY: Readonly<Record<DomainEntityType, DomainEntityDescriptor>> =
  Object.freeze({
    character: Object.freeze({
      domain: 'character',
      label: '[1] CHARACTER DOSSIER (char_)',
      idPrefix: 'char_',
      directoryName: 'characters',
    }),
    location: Object.freeze({
      domain: 'location',
      label: '[2] LOCATION TOPOLOGY (loc_)',
      idPrefix: 'loc_',
      directoryName: 'locations',
    }),
    possession: Object.freeze({
      domain: 'possession',
      label: '[3] POSSESSION ARTIFACT (item_)',
      idPrefix: 'item_',
      directoryName: 'possessions',
    }),
    grievance: Object.freeze({
      domain: 'grievance',
      label: '[4] GRIEVANCE VECTOR (grv_)',
      idPrefix: 'grv_',
      directoryName: 'grievances',
    }),
    world: Object.freeze({
      domain: 'world',
      label: '[5] WORLD AXIOM LAW (law_)',
      idPrefix: 'law_',
      directoryName: 'world',
    }),
  });

export const DOMAIN_ENTITY_CHOICES: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY.character.label,
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY.location.label,
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY.possession.label,
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY.grievance.label,
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY.world.label,
]);

export function resolveDomainEntityDescriptor(selection: string): DomainEntityDescriptor | null {
  const normalized = String(selection || '').trim();
  for (const descriptor of Object.values(DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY)) {
    if (
      normalized === descriptor.label ||
      normalized.toLowerCase() === descriptor.domain
    ) {
      return descriptor;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export default interface DomainSelectorBit {
  readonly idx: string;
  readonly src?: string;
  readonly val?: number;
  readonly dat?: {
    readonly defaultDomain?: DomainEntityType;
    readonly descriptor?: DomainEntityDescriptor;
  };
  readonly slv?: Function;
}
```

#### 2. Domain Entity Type Selector Buzzer: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/domainEntitySelector.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type DomainSelectorBit from '../fce/domainSelector.bit.js';
import {
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_CHOICES,
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY,
  resolveDomainEntityDescriptor,
  type DomainEntityType,
} from '../fce/domainSelector.bit.js';

const UPDATE_GRID = '[Grid action] Update Grid';
const OPEN_CHOICE = '[Open action] Open Choice';
const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console';
const CANCEL_LABEL = '[<] CANCEL DOMAIN SELECTION';

function resolveLibraryBus(): any {
  return (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY || null;
}

function emitConsole(ste: State | undefined, line: string): void {
  const cleanLine = String(line || '').replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '').trim();
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
    return;
  }
  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: cleanLine });
  }
}

export const promptDomainEntityTypeSelector = async (
  cpy: any,
  bal: DomainSelectorBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<any> => {
  const lib = resolveLibraryBus();
  const defaultDomain: DomainEntityType = bal?.dat?.defaultDomain || 'character';
  const defaultDescriptor = DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY[defaultDomain];

  emitConsole(ste, '>> [DOMAIN:SELECTOR] Opening canonical entity domain selector...');

  let selectedRaw = bal?.src || defaultDescriptor.label;

  if (!bal?.src && lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    const gridBit: any = await lib.hunt(UPDATE_GRID, {
      x: 0,
      y: 4,
      xSpan: 4,
      ySpan: 8,
    });
    const choiceBit: any = await lib.hunt(OPEN_CHOICE, {
      dat: { clr0: 'black', clr1: 'cyan' },
      src: 'vertical',
      lst: [...DOMAIN_ENTITY_CHOICES, CANCEL_LABEL],
      net: gridBit?.grdBit?.dat,
    });

    if (choiceBit?.chcBit?.val === -1 || choiceBit?.chcBit?.src === CANCEL_LABEL) {
      emitConsole(ste, '>> [DOMAIN:SELECTOR] [WARN] Entity domain selection cancelled by operator.');
      if (bal?.slv) {
        bal.slv({
          mnuBit: {
            idx: 'domain-selector-cancelled',
            val: 0,
          },
        });
      }
      return cpy;
    }

    selectedRaw = choiceBit?.chcBit?.src || defaultDescriptor.label;
  }

  const resolved = resolveDomainEntityDescriptor(selectedRaw);

  if (!resolved) {
    emitConsole(ste, `:: [DOMAIN:SELECTOR] [FAIL] Invalid entity domain selection: ${selectedRaw}`);
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        mnuBit: {
          idx: 'domain-selector-invalid',
          val: -1,
          src: selectedRaw,
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  cpy.activeEntityDomain = resolved.domain;
  cpy.activeEntityPrefix = resolved.idPrefix;
  cpy.activeEntityDirectory = resolved.directoryName;
  cpy.statusMessage = `Domain Locked: ${resolved.domain} (${resolved.idPrefix})`;

  emitConsole(
    ste,
    `>> [DOMAIN:SELECTOR] [OK] Bound domain '${resolved.domain}' (prefix: ${resolved.idPrefix}, dir: ${resolved.directoryName}/)`,
  );

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: 'domain-selector-success',
        val: 1,
        src: resolved.domain,
        dat: { descriptor: resolved },
      },
    });
  }

  return cpy;
};
```

#### 6. Unit Verification Suite: `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/test/domainEntitySelector.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import {
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_CHOICES,
  DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY,
  resolveDomainEntityDescriptor,
} from '../fce/domainSelector.bit.js';
import { promptDomainEntityTypeSelector } from '../buz/domainEntitySelector.buzz.js';

describe('299.lore - [Feature 1.2] Domain Entity Type Selector', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    delete (globalThis as any).LIBRARY;
    delete (global as any).LIBRARY;
  });

  it('maps all five canonical entity domains to exact prefixes and directories', () => {
    expect(resolveDomainEntityDescriptor(DOMAIN_ENTITY_CHOICES[0])).toEqual(
      DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY.character,
    );
    expect(resolveDomainEntityDescriptor('location')?.idPrefix).toBe('loc_');
    expect(resolveDomainEntityDescriptor('possession')?.idPrefix).toBe('item_');
    expect(resolveDomainEntityDescriptor('grievance')?.idPrefix).toBe('grv_');
    expect(resolveDomainEntityDescriptor('world')?.idPrefix).toBe('law_');
    expect(resolveDomainEntityDescriptor('unknown_domain')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('binds selected entity domain via Blessed OPEN_CHOICE modal and emits 7-bit ASCII telemetry', async () => {
    const telemetry: string[] = [];
    (globalThis as any).LIBRARY = {
      hunt: async (action: string, payload: any) => {
        if (action === '[Console action] Update Console') {
          telemetry.push(payload.src);
          return {};
        }
        if (action === '[Grid action] Update Grid') {
          return { grdBit: { dat: { left: '0%', top: '33%', width: '33%', height: '66%' } } };
        }
        if (action === '[Open action] Open Choice') {
          return { chcBit: { src: DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY.grievance.label, val: 3 } };
        }
        return {};
      },
    };

    const model: any = {};
    let receipt: any = null;

    await promptDomainEntityTypeSelector(model, {
      idx: 'test-domain-select',
      slv: (res: any) => {
        receipt = res;
      },
    });

    expect(model.activeEntityDomain).toBe('grievance');
    expect(model.activeEntityPrefix).toBe('grv_');
    expect(model.activeEntityDirectory).toBe('grievances');
    expect(receipt?.mnuBit?.val).toBe(1);
    expect(telemetry.some((line) => line.includes('[OK] Bound domain \'grievance\''))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('aborts cleanly without mutating model when operator cancels selection', async () => {
    (globalThis as any).LIBRARY = {
      hunt: async (action: string) => {
        if (action === '[Grid action] Update Grid') {
          return { grdBit: { dat: {} } };
        }
        if (action === '[Open action] Open Choice') {
          return { chcBit: { src: '[<] CANCEL DOMAIN SELECTION', val: -1 } };
        }
        return {};
      },
    };

    const model: any = { activeEntityDomain: undefined };
    let receipt: any = null;

    await promptDomainEntityTypeSelector(model, {
      idx: 'test-domain-cancel',
      slv: (res: any) => {
        receipt = res;
      },
    });

    expect(model.activeEntityDomain).toBeUndefined();
    expect(receipt?.mnuBit?.val).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### **User Story**

The showrunner peered into the Blessed terminal flight deck of `000.goblin-lore`, ready to populate a new urban vigilante universe with heroes, rooftop arenas, signature artifacts, and escalating rivalries. Previously, worldbuilders had to remember exact snake_case folder names and prefix conventions (`char_`, `loc_`, `item_`, `grv_`, `law_`) before scaffolding a single entity, risking malformed file paths or broken foreign key anchors. With **[Feature 1.2] Domain Entity Type Selector**, the operator invokes the canonical entity domain selector inside the 12x12 Blessed curses cockpit, choosing from five structured domain categories (`character`, `location`, `possession`, `grievance`, `world`) in a single keystroke. Instantly, the menu controller locks the target subdirectory and snake_case identifier prefix into `MenuModel` and streams pure 7-bit ASCII telemetry to `cns00`, guaranteeing deterministic entity scaffolding at $T_0 = \$0.00$.

#### **Strategic Goal**

Supports the core architectural goal of **Deterministic Entity Taxonomy & Prefix Alignment at $T_0 = \$0.00$**, ensuring all newly authored storyworld dossiers in `000.goblin-lore` adhere strictly to canonical domain directories (`characters/`, `locations/`, `possessions/`, `grievances/`, `world/`) and foreign-key prefixes (`char_`, `loc_`, `item_`, `grv_`, `law_`) prior to compilation.

#### **Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Eliminates manual path-entry errors and prefix mismatches during entity authoring by coupling an interactive `85.choice.unit` selector directly to an immutable domain registry (`DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY`).
- **The Technical Challenge:** Mounting a left-pane Blessed `OPEN_CHOICE` modal (`{ x: 0, y: 4, xSpan: 4, ySpan: 8 }`) inside `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/` that resolves domain descriptors cleanly across both interactive terminal sessions and headless Vitest runs while preserving pure 7-bit ASCII cleanliness, NodeNext `.js` import specifiers, and the immutable `apps/995.library/` harness boundary.

#### **Interface**

- **`000.goblin-lore` (`packages/299.lore`):** Coordinates internal unit contracts across `98.menu.unit/fce/domainSelector.bit.ts` (`DOMAIN_ENTITY_REGISTRY`, `DOMAIN_ENTITY_CHOICES`, `resolveDomainEntityDescriptor`), `98.menu.unit/buz/domainEntitySelector.buzz.ts` (`promptDomainEntityTypeSelector`), `98.menu.unit/menu.action.ts` (`SELECT_DOMAIN_ENTITY_TYPE`), `98.menu.unit/menu.reduce.ts`, and `98.menu.unit/menu.buzzer.ts`.

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### **User Story**

The showrunner peered into the Blessed terminal flight deck of `000.goblin-lore`, preparing to launch a new narrative universe where high-altitude urban vigilantes patrol rain-slicked rooftops rather than goblins scavenging beneath floorboards. Previously, every newly initialized series was locked into a single, rigid crawlspace farce template (`LOW_FANTASY_FARCE`), forcing worldbuilders to manually rewrite configuration schemas before a single character could take a breath. With **Genre Classification Taxonomy Expansion**, the operator enters the Universal Series Genesis Wizard inside the 12x12 Blessed curses cockpit and selects from an expanded palette of Tri-Axial genre archetypes—including `SUPERHERO_URBAN_VIGILANTE` and `STANDARD` alongside `LOW_FANTASY_FARCE`, `SOUTHERN_GOTHIC_INDUSTRIAL`, and `HARD_SCIFI_VACUUM`. In a single keystroke, the compiler calibrates the universe's environmental tone (`tone_bps`), sensory physics (`physics_bps`), temporal pacing (`pacing_bps`), and Lubomír Doležel modal chamber focus (`ALETHIC`, `DEONTIC`, `AXIOLOGICAL`, `EPISTEMIC`), sealing an incorruptible legislative foundation at $T_0 = \$0.00$.

#### **Strategic Goal**

Supports the core architectural goal of Meta-Ontology vs. Story Instance decoupling ($\langle \mathcal{S}, \mathcal{A}, \mathcal{I}, \mathcal{R}_m \rangle$) and extensible multi-genre storyworld parameterization across the sovereign monorepo fleet without runtime schema drift or floating-point arithmetic corruption.

#### **User Story**

The showrunner peered into the Blessed terminal flight deck of `000.goblin-lore`, ready to assign physical limb capacities and 2d6 combat statistics to a newly drafted Gotham vigilante dossier. Previously, floating-point scalars like `0.5` arms or out-of-range combat attributes could slip into character configurations, causing downstream physics engines and inventory validators to miscalculate hand loads or permit an injured hero with one free arm to wield two two-handed artifacts simultaneously. With **[Feature 2.2] Integer Basis Point Capacity, Stat Configuration**, every somatic capacity metric (`arms_available_bps`, `occupies_hands_bps`) is strictly enforced as a discrete integer basis point ($0 \le x \le 10{,}000\text{ bp}$), while 2d6 combat statistics (`spite`, `cunning`, `vigor`, `poise`) are clamped to integer bounds `[1, 5]`.
